| HEAD-0001 | 17 | /html[1]/head[1]/title[1]/text()[1] | "Lotte World Seoul Guide 2026: Tickets, Magic Pass, Rides & Tips" |
| SCHEMA-0001 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/0/name | "Is one day enough for Lotte World Seoul?" |
| SCHEMA-0002 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/0/acceptedAnswer/text | "Yes, for most visitors one full day is enough to have a good Lotte World experience, but not necessarily enough to ride everything. Long queues can quickly reduce how many major attractions you complete, so choose your 3–5 must-rides first." |
| SCHEMA-0003 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/1/name | "Is After4 worth it?" |
| SCHEMA-0004 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/1/acceptedAnswer/text | "After4 can be good value if you want a shorter evening visit, only have a few ride priorities or care as much about the atmosphere as the number of rides. If this is your only visit and several headline rides are non-negotiable, a full-day ticket is usually the safer choice." |
| SCHEMA-0005 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/2/name | "Is Magic Pass worth it?" |
| SCHEMA-0006 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/2/acceptedAnswer/text | "It depends on your must-rides, the expected queues and how much you value your time. Magic Pass makes more sense when several rides you genuinely care about are eligible and likely to have long waits. It is much less useful if your priorities are flexible or younger children cannot use many of the covered rides." |
| SCHEMA-0007 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/3/name | "Can Magic Pass sell out?" |
| SCHEMA-0008 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/3/acceptedAnswer/text | "Yes. Magic Pass is sold in limited quantities and can sell out. If it is important to your plan, check the current official purchase rules before your visit rather than assuming you can buy it later in the day." |
| SCHEMA-0009 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/4/name | "Is Lotte World good for young children?" |
| SCHEMA-0010 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/4/acceptedAnswer/text | "Yes, but plan differently from a thrill-ride visit. Check height restrictions first, use the family and children's attractions that fit your group, and build in real breaks. For younger children, managing energy usually matters more than maximizing ride count." |
| SCHEMA-0011 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/5/name | "Is Lotte World mostly indoors?" |
| SCHEMA-0012 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/5/acceptedAnswer/text | "Adventure is a large indoor theme park, but Magic Island is outdoors and includes several major thrill rides. Rain, wind or extreme temperatures can therefore still affect your visit, especially if your must-rides are on Magic Island." |
| SCHEMA-0013 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/6/name | "What are the best rides at Lotte World?" |
| SCHEMA-0014 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/6/acceptedAnswer/text | "There is no single best list for everyone. Thrill-focused visitors often prioritize rides such as Atlantis, Comet Express, French Revolution and the Gyro attractions, while families may choose very differently after checking height restrictions. The better question is which 3–5 rides would make your own visit feel successful." |
| SCHEMA-0015 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/7/name | "Can I visit Lotte World, Seoul Sky and the Aquarium on the same day?" |
| SCHEMA-0016 | 282 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/7/acceptedAnswer/text | "You can, but it is usually too much for a ride-focused Lotte World day. If Lotte World is your main reason for visiting Jamsil, give it the day and add Seoul Sky or the Aquarium only if you intentionally want a shorter theme-park visit." |

| MAIN-0009 | 430 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "If rides are the reason you are coming to Lotte World, treat it as the main event and give it the day. | MAIN-0019 | 442 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "With younger children, do not measure a good day by how many attractions you complete. Height restrictions, queues, meals, noise and simple fatigue can matter more than squeezing in one more ride." |
| MAIN-0020 | 443 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "And just because the Aquarium, Seoul Sky and Seokchon Lake are next door does not mean they all belong in the same itinerary. If Lotte World is the reason you came to Jamsil, protect the time you came for." |

| MAIN-0081 | 537 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[4]/text()[1] | "What is included — and what is not" |
| MAIN-0082 | 538 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/text()[1] | "The standard Lotte World ticket gives you admission to Lotte World Adventure and use of regular attractions, excluding separately paid facilities such as arcade-style games. The standard comprehensive ticket also includes admission to the Folk Museum." |
| MAIN-0083 | 539 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[1] | "The Folk Museum is useful if you genuinely want to visit it, but for most ride-focused travelers it should not influence the main ticket decision. The bigger question remains how much park time you need." |
| MAIN-0084 | 540 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/text()[1] | "More importantly:" |
| MAIN-0085 | 541 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Magic Pass is not included in your normal admission ticket." |
| MAIN-0086 | 542 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[19]/text()[1] | "It is a separate limited-quantity product. Buying a Lotte World admission ticket does not give you priority access to rides." |
| MAIN-0087 | 543 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[20]/text()[1] | "We deal with Magic Pass later in this guide because the right order is:" |
| MAIN-0088 | 544 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[21]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Choose your visit length → choose your must-rides → then decide whether a Magic Pass is worth paying for." |
| MAIN-0089 | 545 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[5]/text()[1] | "Check your ticket price before you book" |
| MAIN-0090 | 546 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[22]/text()[1] | "Lotte World frequently runs its own discounts, while Trip.com and other attraction platforms may offer a different ticket price or package for the same date." |
| MAIN-0091 | 547 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[23]/text()[1] | "Compare:" |

| MAIN-0098 | 566 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Do not choose a ticket simply because it has the biggest percentage discount. Choose the ticket that gives you enough time for the day you are actually planning." |
| MAIN-0099 | 575 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Adventure vs Magic Island: Decide Where Your Day Starts" |
| MAIN-0100 | 578 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Lotte World is split between" |
| MAIN-0101 | 578 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Adventure" |
| MAIN-0102 | 578 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | ", the large indoor park, and" |
| MAIN-0103 | 578 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "Magic Island" |
| MAIN-0104 | 578 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[3] | ", the outdoor section on Seokchon Lake." |
| MAIN-0105 | 579 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "You can move between them during the day, but that does not mean you should keep crossing back and forth every time a ride catches your attention on the map." |
| ATTR-0009 | 582 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/div[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Lotte World Adventure indoor park in Seoul" |
| ATTR-0010 | 583 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/div[1]/img[2]/@alt | "Lotte World Magic Island beside Seokchon Lake" |
| MAIN-0106 | 585 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0107 | 587 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Your first question should be simpler:" |
| MAIN-0108 | 588 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Where are the rides you care about most?" |
| MAIN-0109 | 589 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Adventure: the safer base when weather is uncertain" |
| MAIN-0110 | 590 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Adventure is the indoor side of Lotte World, covered by a huge glass dome." |
| MAIN-0111 | 591 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "It includes rides, performances, family attractions and several of the park's major indoor experiences. It is also the more reliable part of your plan when rain, strong wind, heat or cold makes the outdoor section less predictable." |
| MAIN-0112 | 592 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Adventure makes a sensible starting point if:" |
| MAIN-0113 | 594 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "most of your must-rides are indoors" |
| MAIN-0114 | 595 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "you are visiting with younger children" |
| MAIN-0115 | 596 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "the weather forecast is poor" |
| MAIN-0116 | 597 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "you want easier access to food and breaks" |
| MAIN-0117 | 598 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[5]/text()[1] | "you would rather build the day around attractions that are less exposed to weather disruption" |
| MAIN-0118 | 600 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "But “indoor” does not mean “short queues.” Popular Adventure rides can still consume a large part of your day." |
| MAIN-0119 | 601 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Magic Island: prioritize it when your must-rides are outside" |
| MAIN-0120 | 602 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "Magic Island sits outside on Seokchon Lake and is reached from Adventure via the second-floor overbridge." |
| MAIN-0121 | 603 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[10]/text()[1] | "This is where several of Lotte World's headline thrill rides are concentrated, including attractions such as Atlantis, Comet Express and the Gyro rides." |
| MAIN-0122 | 604 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[11]/text()[1] | "If two or three of your highest-priority rides are on Magic Island, do not leave them until late in the day simply because Adventure is the first area you see." |
| MAIN-0123 | 605 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[1] | "Check the weather and operating status, then decide whether Magic Island deserves your first serious block of ride time." |
| MAIN-0124 | 606 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Weather can change the value of your plan" |
| MAIN-0125 | 607 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[13]/text()[1] | "Magic Island is more exposed to weather." |
| MAIN-0126 | 608 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[1] | "Some outdoor attractions can suspend operation because of rain, wind, high or low temperatures, or on-site operating conditions. That matters because a ride you planned to “come back for later” may not still be operating when you return." |
| MAIN-0127 | 609 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/text()[1] | "If the forecast looks unstable and an outdoor ride is one of your must-rides, consider moving it earlier rather than treating it as an end-of-day extra." |
| MAIN-0128 | 610 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[1] | "At the same time, do not rush outside just because Magic Island has famous rides. If the rides you actually care about are indoors, there is no prize for following somebody else's attraction ranking." |
| MAIN-0129 | 611 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[4]/text()[1] | "Do not waste the day crossing between zones" |
| MAIN-0130 | 612 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/text()[1] | "Adventure and Magic Island are connected, but repeated backtracking still costs time." |
| MAIN-0131 | 613 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Group your rides by zone, start where your priorities and the weather make the most sense, and avoid unnecessary backtracking." |
| MAIN-0132 | 614 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[19]/text()[1] | "A reason to switch zones might be a specific ride, a meal, a performance, bad weather or simply needing a break." |
| MAIN-0133 | 615 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[20]/text()[1] | "It should not be:" |
| MAIN-0134 | 616 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[21]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "“We saw something else on the map.”" |
| MAIN-0135 | 617 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[22]/text()[1] | "Before you enter, mark your 3–5 must-rides and note which side of the park they are on." |
| MAIN-0136 | 618 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[23]/text()[1] | "That one step will tell you far more about where to start than a generic list of the “best rides.”" |
| MAIN-0137 | 626 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "What Rides Are at Lotte World?" |
| MAIN-0138 | 629 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "The point of this list is not to turn Lotte World into a checklist. It is to help you understand what is actually in the park before you choose the few rides that deserve priority." |
| MAIN-0139 | 630 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Magic Island — thrill rides first" |
| ATTR-0011 | 632 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Outdoor thrill ride on Lotte World Magic Island" |

| MAIN-0148 | 646 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "→ A major candidate for a thrill-focused day and easy to group with Atlantis in a Magic Island block. Current official guidance lists 120 cm+." |

| MAIN-0283 | 863 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "These are the rides most likely to shape a thrill-focused Lotte World day." |
| MAIN-0284 | 864 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "If Atlantis and Comet Express are high priorities, give Magic Island serious time. If the weather is poor, French Revolution gives you a major indoor thrill option." |
| MAIN-0285 | 865 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "You probably do not need all five as must-rides. Pick the" |
| MAIN-0286 | 865 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "three that matter most" |
| MAIN-0287 | 865 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[2] | ", then treat the others as upgrades if the queues cooperate." |
| MAIN-0288 | 866 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Families With Young Kids" |
| MAIN-0289 | 867 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Start with:" |

| MAIN-0292 | 871 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Jumping Fish" |

| MAIN-0295 | 874 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[6]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Children's Story Theater" |
| MAIN-0296 | 876 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "Add gentler family rides such as" |
| MAIN-0297 | 876 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Camelot Carrousel" |
| MAIN-0298 | 876 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[2] | "or" |
| MAIN-0299 | 876 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "Balloon Ride" |
| MAIN-0300 | 876 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[3] | "if they fit your child." |

| MAIN-0301 | 879 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0302 | 881 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "Do not begin with a list of famous rides and then discover that your child is too short to ride them." |
| MAIN-0303 | 882 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[10]/text()[1] | "Check height restrictions first, then build the day around what the youngest child can actually enjoy." |
| MAIN-0304 | 883 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[11]/text()[1] | "For this group, a successful day may involve fewer headline attractions and more short rides, shows and breaks." |
| MAIN-0305 | 884 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Older Kids and Teens" |
| MAIN-0306 | 885 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[1] | "A mixed family with older children or teenagers can usually combine stronger rides with group-friendly attractions." |
| MAIN-0307 | 886 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[13]/text()[1] | "Good starting candidates include:" |
| MAIN-0308 | 888 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Atlantis" |
| MAIN-0309 | 889 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Comet Express" |
| MAIN-0310 | 890 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "French Revolution" |
| MAIN-0311 | 891 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Flume Ride" |
| MAIN-0312 | 892 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "The Adventure of Sindbad" |
| MAIN-0313 | 893 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[6]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Bumper Car" |
| MAIN-0314 | 894 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[7]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "3D Desperados 2" |
| MAIN-0315 | 896 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[1] | "This group has more flexibility than families with small children but does not need to turn the day into a thrill-ride marathon." |
| MAIN-0316 | 897 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/text()[1] | "Let each person choose one or two priorities, then look for overlap." |
| MAIN-0317 | 898 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[4]/text()[1] | "If You Do Not Like Big Drops or Strong Spinning" |
| MAIN-0318 | 899 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[1] | "Lotte World is still worth considering even if roller coasters and drop rides are not your thing." |
| MAIN-0319 | 900 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/text()[1] | "Look at:" |
| MAIN-0320 | 902 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[4]/li[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Balloon Ride" |
| MAIN-0321 | 903 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[4]/li[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "World Monorail" |
| MAIN-0322 | 904 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[4]/li[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Camelot Carrousel" |
| MAIN-0323 | 905 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[4]/li[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "The Adventure of Sindbad" |
| MAIN-0324 | 906 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[4]/li[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Fantasy Dream" |
| MAIN-0325 | 907 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[4]/li[6]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Dream Boat" |
| MAIN-0326 | 908 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[4]/li[7]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Children's Story Theater" |
| MAIN-0327 | 910 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/text()[1] | "Interactive attractions such as" |
| MAIN-0328 | 910 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "3D Desperados 2" |
| MAIN-0329 | 910 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/text()[2] | "or" |
| MAIN-0330 | 910 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "4D Shooting Theater" |
| MAIN-0331 | 910 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/text()[3] | "can also give you something more active without committing to a major coaster." |
| MAIN-0332 | 911 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[19]/text()[1] | "Do not let somebody else's “best rides” list convince you that you need to spend the day on attractions you already know you dislike." |
| MAIN-0333 | 912 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[5]/text()[1] | "Rainy-Day or Bad-Weather Picks" |
| MAIN-0334 | 913 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[20]/text()[1] | "When Magic Island becomes less reliable, shift attention indoors." |
| MAIN-0335 | 914 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[21]/text()[1] | "Useful options include:" |
| MAIN-0336 | 916 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[5]/li[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "French Revolution" |
| MAIN-0337 | 917 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[5]/li[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Flume Ride" |
| MAIN-0338 | 918 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[5]/li[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Pharaoh's Fury" |
| MAIN-0339 | 919 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[5]/li[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "The Adventure of Sindbad" |
| MAIN-0340 | 920 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[5]/li[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Wild Jungle" |
| MAIN-0341 | 921 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[5]/li[6]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Wild Valley" |
| MAIN-0342 | 922 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[5]/li[7]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "3D Desperados 2" |
| MAIN-0343 | 923 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[5]/li[8]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Balloon Ride" |
| MAIN-0344 | 925 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[22]/text()[1] | "Bad weather does not mean you have to abandon Lotte World." |
| MAIN-0345 | 926 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[23]/text()[1] | "It does mean that an itinerary built around Atlantis, Gyro Swing and other outdoor priorities may need to change quickly." |
| MAIN-0346 | 934 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Pick Your 3–5 Must-Rides First" |
| MAIN-0347 | 937 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Before you look at Magic Pass tiers or try to optimize the whole park, choose the" |
| MAIN-0348 | 937 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "3–5 rides you would genuinely be disappointed to miss" |
| MAIN-0349 | 937 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | "." |
| MAIN-0350 | 938 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "This is the simplest way to stop long queues from controlling your day." |
| MAIN-0351 | 939 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Your list does not need to match somebody else's “Top 10 rides at Lotte World.” A thrill-ride fan, a family with younger children and someone visiting mostly for photos should not have the same priorities." |
| MAIN-0352 | 940 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Start with three categories" |
| MAIN-0353 | 941 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Divide the rides you are considering into:" |
| MAIN-0354 | 942 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Must-ride" |
| MAIN-0355 | 942 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "You would accept a long wait or change the rest of the schedule to protect it." |
| MAIN-0356 | 944 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Would-like-to-ride" |
| MAIN-0357 | 944 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "You want to do it, but only if the wait fits the day." |
| MAIN-0358 | 946 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Bonus" |
| MAIN-0359 | 946 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "You will ride it if the timing is good. You will not reorganize the day for it." |
| MAIN-0360 | 948 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "Only the first group should drive your route." |
| MAIN-0361 | 949 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Keep the must-ride list short" |
| MAIN-0362 | 950 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "Three priorities is comfortable." |
| MAIN-0363 | 951 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[10]/text()[1] | "Five is still workable." |
| MAIN-0364 | 952 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[11]/text()[1] | "Once seven or eight rides become “must-do,” you no longer have a priority list. You have a checklist that depends on every queue cooperating." |
| MAIN-0365 | 953 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[1] | "That is exactly when one 90-minute wait can start pushing meals, breaks and other rides later and later." |
| MAIN-0366 | 954 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Choose for your group, not for the internet" |
| MAIN-0367 | 955 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[13]/text()[1] | "A famous ride is not automatically worth protecting." |
| MAIN-0368 | 956 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[1] | "For example, a thrill-focused visitor may build the day around attractions such as" |
| MAIN-0369 | 956 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Atlantis, Comet Express or French Revolution" |
| MAIN-0370 | 956 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[2] | "." |
| MAIN-0371 | 957 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/text()[1] | "A family with younger children may have a completely different list after checking height restrictions." |
| MAIN-0372 | 958 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[1] | "Someone who dislikes intense rides may decide that performances, gentler attractions and the atmosphere matter more than any headline coaster." |
| MAIN-0373 | 959 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/text()[1] | "The correct list is the one that would make" |
| MAIN-0374 | 959 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "your" |
| MAIN-0375 | 959 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/text()[2] | "visit feel successful." |
| MAIN-0376 | 960 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[4]/text()[1] | "Check where your priorities are located" |
| MAIN-0377 | 961 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/text()[1] | "Once you have your 3–5 rides, mark whether they are in" |
| MAIN-0378 | 961 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Adventure or Magic Island" |
| MAIN-0379 | 961 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/text()[2] | "." |
| MAIN-0380 | 962 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[19]/text()[1] | "If most are on Magic Island, that is a strong reason to give the outdoor section an early block of time, especially when the weather is uncertain." |
| MAIN-0381 | 963 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[20]/text()[1] | "If most are indoors, there is little reason to rush outside simply because the outdoor thrill rides appear on every “best rides” list." |
| MAIN-0382 | 964 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[21]/text()[1] | "Your ride list should determine the route — not the other way around." |
| MAIN-0383 | 965 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[5]/text()[1] | "Give yourself one backup" |
| MAIN-0384 | 966 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[22]/text()[1] | "At least one of your priorities should have a practical substitute." |
| MAIN-0385 | 967 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[23]/text()[1] | "An outdoor ride may stop because of weather. A major attraction may temporarily close. A queue may suddenly become much longer than expected." |
| MAIN-0386 | 968 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[24]/text()[1] | "If every ride on your list is a non-negotiable headline attraction, you have no room to adapt." |
| MAIN-0387 | 969 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[25]/text()[1] | "A better plan is:" |
| MAIN-0388 | 970 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[26]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "3–5 protected priorities + several flexible backups." |
| MAIN-0389 | 971 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[6]/text()[1] | "Now you are ready to think about Magic Pass" |
| MAIN-0390 | 972 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[27]/text()[1] | "Do not buy a Magic Pass simply because Lotte World is busy." |
| MAIN-0391 | 973 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[28]/text()[1] | "First ask:" |
| MAIN-0392 | 975 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "Which rides are genuinely important to me?" |
| MAIN-0393 | 976 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "Which of those rides are eligible for Magic Pass?" |
| MAIN-0394 | 977 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "Which Magic Pass tier covers them?" |
| MAIN-0395 | 978 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "How much time could I realistically save?" |
| MAIN-0396 | 979 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[5]/text()[1] | "Is that time worth the extra cost for my group?" |
| MAIN-0397 | 981 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[29]/text()[1] | "That is why the ride list comes before the pass." |
| MAIN-0398 | 982 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[30]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Choose the rides first. Choose the Magic Pass second." |
| MAIN-0399 | 984 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Know the rides you want? Check the ticket price for your visit date before you keep planning." |
| MAIN-0400 | 994 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[2]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Check Lotte World tickets on Trip.com" |
| MAIN-0401 | 1003 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "The Queue Reality at Lotte World" |
| MAIN-0402 | 1006 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "The biggest mistake at Lotte World is assuming you can decide what to ride as you go." |
| MAIN-0403 | 1007 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Popular attractions can build queues of an hour or more, even on weekdays. On crowded days, some waits can stretch much longer." |
| MAIN-0404 | 1008 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "That changes the way you should plan the park." |
| MAIN-0405 | 1009 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "A 90-minute queue is not just 90 minutes. Add the walk to the attraction, the ride itself, finding your next destination, meals and breaks, and one decision can take a large piece of your day." |
| ATTR-0014 | 1011 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Visitors waiting near an outdoor ride at Lotte World" |
| MAIN-0406 | 1012 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0407 | 1014 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "A weekday does not guarantee short lines" |
| MAIN-0408 | 1015 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Weekdays are generally a better bet than weekends and major holidays, but they are not a guarantee of short waits." |
| MAIN-0409 | 1016 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "Recent visitors have reported 60-minute-plus waits across several major rides even on weekdays." |
| MAIN-0410 | 1017 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "So do not build a plan that only works if the park is quiet." |
| MAIN-0411 | 1018 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "Build one that still works when it is busy." |
| MAIN-0412 | 1019 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Decide what is worth waiting for before you arrive" |
| MAIN-0413 | 1020 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "Not every popular ride deserves the same amount of your time." |
| MAIN-0414 | 1021 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[10]/text()[1] | "If Atlantis is one of the main reasons you came, a long queue may be a trade you are willing to make." |
| MAIN-0415 | 1022 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[11]/text()[1] | "If another ride is only a nice-to-have, the same wait may be a reason to walk away." |
| MAIN-0416 | 1023 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[1] | "That distinction should be made before you are standing underneath a 90-minute wait-time sign." |
| MAIN-0417 | 1024 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[13]/text()[1] | "Ask yourself:" |
| MAIN-0418 | 1025 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Which rides would I still wait for if the queue reached an hour?" |
| MAIN-0419 | 1026 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/text()[1] | "Those are your real priorities." |
| MAIN-0420 | 1027 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[1] | "Everything else should be easier to drop." |
| MAIN-0421 | 1028 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Set a point where you walk away" |
| MAIN-0422 | 1029 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/text()[1] | "You do not need one universal rule for every ride." |
| MAIN-0423 | 1030 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/text()[1] | "But you do need a limit." |
| MAIN-0424 | 1031 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[19]/text()[1] | "For a must-ride, you may accept a long standby wait." |
| MAIN-0425 | 1032 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[20]/text()[1] | "For a second-tier ride, the same wait may cost too much of the day." |
| MAIN-0426 | 1033 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[21]/text()[1] | "The important part is deciding that before queue pressure takes over your schedule." |
| MAIN-0427 | 1034 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[22]/text()[1] | "Once every attraction becomes “we have already come this far, so we may as well wait,” Lotte World can turn into a day of standing in lines rather than riding." |
| MAIN-0428 | 1035 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[4]/text()[1] | "Do not chase every famous ride" |
| MAIN-0429 | 1036 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[23]/text()[1] | "A list of the park's most popular rides is easy to find." |
| MAIN-0430 | 1037 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[24]/text()[1] | "Trying to complete all of them is a different problem." |
| MAIN-0431 | 1038 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[25]/text()[1] | "The more headline attractions you make non-negotiable, the more your entire schedule depends on queue lengths you cannot control." |
| MAIN-0432 | 1039 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[26]/text()[1] | "For most visitors, a better target is:" |
| MAIN-0433 | 1040 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[27]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "3–5 rides that genuinely matter, then everything else is a bonus." |
| MAIN-0434 | 1041 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[28]/text()[1] | "That gives you room for a meal, a break, bad weather, a temporary ride closure or simply changing your mind." |
| MAIN-0435 | 1042 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[5]/text()[1] | "A long queue should change the plan — not destroy it" |
| MAIN-0436 | 1043 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[29]/text()[1] | "If one of your priorities suddenly shows a very long wait, you have options:" |
| MAIN-0437 | 1045 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "wait because it is genuinely one of your must-rides" |
| MAIN-0438 | 1046 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "move to another priority and check again later" |
| MAIN-0439 | 1047 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "use Magic Pass if the ride and your pass are eligible" |
| MAIN-0440 | 1048 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "replace it with an indoor or lower-wait alternative" |
| MAIN-0441 | 1049 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[5]/text()[1] | "skip it entirely" |
| MAIN-0442 | 1051 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[30]/text()[1] | "Skipping a ride is not a failed day." |
| MAIN-0443 | 1052 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[31]/text()[1] | "Spending half the afternoon protecting a ride you did not care about very much is the bigger mistake." |
| MAIN-0444 | 1053 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[32]/text()[1] | "The next step is therefore not to make a longer ride list." |
| MAIN-0445 | 1054 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[33]/text()[1] | "It is to choose the" |
| MAIN-0446 | 1054 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[33]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "3–5 rides worth protecting your day for." |
| MAIN-0447 | 1062 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Ride Priority Strategy: Protect the Rides That Are Hardest to Recover Later" |
| MAIN-0448 | 1065 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "There is no single “best ride order” that works for everyone at Lotte World." |
| MAIN-0449 | 1066 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Your priority should be the rides that are" |
| MAIN-0450 | 1066 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "both important to you and difficult to recover later" |
| MAIN-0451 | 1066 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | "if the queue becomes very long, the weather changes or the attraction temporarily stops operating." |
| MAIN-0452 | 1067 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "That usually matters more than following somebody else's Top 10 ranking." |
| ATTR-0015 | 1069 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Outdoor roller coaster and Magic Castle at Lotte World" |
| MAIN-0453 | 1070 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0454 | 1072 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "If thrill rides are the main reason you came" |
| MAIN-0455 | 1073 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "If Atlantis, Comet Express or the Gyro rides are among your must-rides, Magic Island deserves serious attention early in the day when the weather is suitable." |
| MAIN-0456 | 1074 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Atlantis and Comet Express are both on Magic Island, so travelers who care about both can protect a large part of their thrill-ride plan without repeatedly crossing back into Adventure." |
| MAIN-0457 | 1075 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "The mistake is spending the first part of the morning on low-priority attractions simply because their queues look short, then reaching your real must-rides after the major waits have already built." |
| MAIN-0458 | 1076 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "A short queue is only good value if you actually care about the ride." |
| MAIN-0459 | 1077 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "If weather is uncertain, keep an indoor priority ready" |
| MAIN-0460 | 1078 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "French Revolution gives thrill-focused visitors a major indoor priority inside Adventure." |
| MAIN-0461 | 1079 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "Other indoor attractions such as Flume Ride and Pharaoh's Fury can also give you alternatives when Magic Island operations become less predictable." |
| MAIN-0462 | 1080 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[10]/text()[1] | "That does not mean indoor attractions will have short queues." |
| MAIN-0463 | 1081 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[11]/text()[1] | "It means bad weather does not have to leave you without a plan." |
| MAIN-0464 | 1082 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Use ride roles instead of trying to rank the whole park" |
| MAIN-0465 | 1083 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[1] | "You do not need another generic Top 10 list." |
| MAIN-0466 | 1084 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[13]/text()[1] | "Think in roles instead:" |
| MAIN-0467 | 1085 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Headline outdoor thrill" |
| MAIN-0468 | 1085 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[1] | "— Atlantis" |
| MAIN-0469 | 1086 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "Outdoor coaster block" |
| MAIN-0470 | 1086 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[3] | "— Comet Express" |
| MAIN-0471 | 1087 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/strong[3]/text()[1] | "Strong spinning/drop thrill" |
| MAIN-0472 | 1087 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[5] | "— Gyro Swing / Gyro Drop" |
| MAIN-0473 | 1088 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/strong[4]/text()[1] | "Indoor thrill backup" |
| MAIN-0474 | 1088 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[7] | "— French Revolution" |
| MAIN-0475 | 1089 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/strong[5]/text()[1] | "Family / medium-intensity indoor option" |
| MAIN-0476 | 1089 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[9] | "— Flume Ride" |
| MAIN-0477 | 1090 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/strong[6]/text()[1] | "Indoor themed backup" |
| MAIN-0478 | 1090 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[11] | "— Pharaoh's Fury" |
| MAIN-0479 | 1091 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/text()[1] | "Someone who dislikes intense rides should not protect Atlantis just because it is famous. A family may remove several of these attractions completely after checking height restrictions." |
| MAIN-0480 | 1092 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[1] | "The point is to give each ride a" |
| MAIN-0481 | 1092 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "job in your plan" |
| MAIN-0482 | 1092 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[2] | "." |
| MAIN-0483 | 1093 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[4]/text()[1] | "Your first two hours should protect something important" |
| MAIN-0484 | 1094 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/text()[1] | "Look at your 3–5 must-rides and ask which one would be hardest to recover if you postpone it." |
| MAIN-0485 | 1095 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/text()[1] | "If several of your priorities are on Magic Island and the weather looks stable, an outdoor block early in the day can make sense." |
| MAIN-0486 | 1096 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[19]/text()[1] | "If your priorities are mostly inside Adventure, or the outdoor forecast is poor, starting indoors may be the better use of your time." |
| MAIN-0487 | 1097 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[20]/text()[1] | "Do not cross the park simply because another attraction temporarily shows a shorter wait." |
| MAIN-0488 | 1098 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[21]/text()[1] | "Protect the plan first. Fill gaps second." |
| MAIN-0489 | 1099 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[5]/text()[1] | "Be careful where you use Magic Pass" |
| MAIN-0490 | 1100 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[22]/text()[1] | "Some major attractions, including Atlantis, Comet Express, French Revolution, Flume Ride and Pharaoh's Fury, cannot be ridden repeatedly with Magic Pass. Magic Pass use for those attractions is limited to one ride per attraction." |
| MAIN-0491 | 1101 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[23]/text()[1] | "That makes the decision more important." |
| MAIN-0492 | 1102 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[24]/text()[1] | "If one of those rides is a must-ride, use the pass because protecting that ride saves meaningful time — not simply because you happen to be standing nearby." |
| MAIN-0493 | 1103 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[25]/text()[1] | "We compare the current Magic Pass tiers next." |
| MAIN-0494 | 1104 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[26]/text()[1] | "For now, remember the same rule:" |
| MAIN-0495 | 1105 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[27]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "The ride should justify the pass. The pass should not decide what you ride." |
| MAIN-0496 | 1113 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Magic Pass 2026: Is It Worth It, and Which Tier Should You Buy?" |
| MAIN-0497 | 1116 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Magic Pass can save a large amount of queue time at Lotte World, but" |
| MAIN-0498 | 1116 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "buying one without matching it to your must-rides is an expensive mistake" |
| MAIN-0499 | 1116 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | "." |
| MAIN-0500 | 1117 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "In 2026, Magic Pass is no longer a simple choice between “a few fast passes” and “more fast passes.” The current system has" |
| MAIN-0501 | 1117 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "four different 5-use tiers" |
| MAIN-0502 | 1117 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | ", and the attractions you can use them on differ by tier." |
| MAIN-0503 | 1118 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "That is why the rule for this guide is:" |
| MAIN-0504 | 1119 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Choose your rides first. Choose your Magic Pass second." |
| MAIN-0505 | 1120 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Current Magic Pass prices" |
| MAIN-0506 | 1121 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Checked: September 15, 2026" |
| MAIN-0507 | 1122 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Light — ₩54,000" |
| MAIN-0508 | 1122 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "5 uses. Start here only if your priorities fit the lower-priced eligible ride group." |
| MAIN-0509 | 1124 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Standard A — ₩65,000" |
| MAIN-0510 | 1124 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "5 uses. More relevant when Atlantis / Flume Ride / Gyro Swing-type priorities matter." |
| MAIN-0511 | 1126 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Standard B — ₩65,000" |
| MAIN-0512 | 1126 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "5 uses. More relevant when Comet Express / French Revolution-type priorities matter." |
| MAIN-0513 | 1128 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Premium — ₩80,000" |
| MAIN-0514 | 1128 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "5 uses. Wider coverage for travelers whose priorities are spread across multiple ride groups." |
| MAIN-0515 | 1130 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[10]/text()[1] | "Do not choose Premium simply because it has the widest coverage." |
| MAIN-0516 | 1131 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[11]/text()[1] | "If the three rides you care about are already covered by Standard A or Standard B, paying more for additional eligible rides that you probably will not use gives you very little extra value." |
| MAIN-0517 | 1132 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[1] | "The exact attraction list can change, so" |
| MAIN-0518 | 1132 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "check the current list in the official Lotte World app before purchasing" |
| MAIN-0519 | 1132 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[2] | "." |
| MAIN-0520 | 1133 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Standard A vs Standard B is a ride decision, not a quality decision" |
| MAIN-0521 | 1134 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[13]/text()[1] | "Standard A and Standard B currently cost the same." |
| MAIN-0522 | 1135 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[14]/text()[1] | "Neither is automatically better." |
| MAIN-0523 | 1136 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/text()[1] | "If" |
| MAIN-0524 | 1136 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Atlantis and Gyro Swing" |
| MAIN-0525 | 1136 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[15]/text()[2] | "are near the top of your list, Standard A is likely to make more sense." |
| MAIN-0526 | 1137 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[1] | "If" |
| MAIN-0527 | 1137 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Comet Express and French Revolution" |
| MAIN-0528 | 1137 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[16]/text()[2] | "matter more, Standard B is the more relevant starting point." |
| MAIN-0529 | 1138 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[17]/text()[1] | "This is exactly why buying Magic Pass before choosing your must-rides gets the decision backwards." |
| MAIN-0530 | 1139 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "When Magic Pass is most likely to be worth the money" |
| MAIN-0531 | 1140 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[18]/text()[1] | "Magic Pass becomes easier to justify when several of these apply:" |
| MAIN-0532 | 1142 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "you are visiting on a busy day" |

| MAIN-0538 | 1148 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[7]/text()[1] | "your selected rides actually match the tier you are buying" |
| MAIN-0539 | 1150 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[19]/text()[1] | "Think about the purchase as" |
| MAIN-0540 | 1150 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[19]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "buying back time" |

| MAIN-0542 | 1151 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[20]/text()[1] | "If avoiding two or three long waits saves enough time for another major ride, a proper meal, a break with your children or simply a less exhausting day, Magic Pass may be worth much more than its face value to you." |

| MAIN-0550 | 1160 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[5]/text()[1] | "you are comfortable abandoning a ride when its queue becomes too long" |

| MAIN-0553 | 1164 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[24]/text()[1] | "Before paying for every person, check whether everyone can — and actually wants to — use the rides that justify the pass." |

| MAIN-0611 | 1214 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[52]/text()[1] | "Ask:" |
| MAIN-0612 | 1215 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[53]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "“How many of my must-rides does this tier protect, and how much time could that realistically save me?”" |
| MAIN-0613 | 1216 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[54]/text()[1] | "If the answer is three or four rides that genuinely matter on a crowded day, the extra cost can make sense." |
| MAIN-0614 | 1217 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[55]/text()[1] | "If the answer is one ride and four attractions you would not have chosen anyway, keep the money." |
| MAIN-0615 | 1218 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[56]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "The ride should justify the pass. The pass should never decide the ride." |

| MAIN-0617 | 1221 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[2]/small[1]/text()[1] | "Magic Pass is separate unless the booking product specifically says otherwise." |

| MAIN-0623 | 1246 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "It is:" |
| MAIN-0624 | 1247 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "“Which rides can everyone actually use, and how long can the children realistically enjoy the park before queues and fatigue take over?”" |

| MAIN-0633 | 1254 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "110 cm or taller" |

| MAIN-0643 | 1261 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[11]/text()[1] | "Then ask whether the eligible rides are important enough to justify the extra cost." |
| MAIN-0644 | 1262 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Do not judge a family day by ride count" |
| MAIN-0645 | 1263 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[1] | "Children experience queues differently from adults." |
| MAIN-0646 | 1264 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[13]/text()[1] | "A 60-minute wait followed by another long queue may be manageable for an adult trying to maximize thrill rides. For a young child, it can turn the rest of the afternoon into damage control." |

| MAIN-0655 | 1274 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[1]/text()[1] | "older family members want a thrill ride that a younger child cannot use" |

| MAIN-0658 | 1277 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[4]/text()[1] | "the group needs to reset without leaving the park" |
| MAIN-0659 | 1279 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[21]/text()[1] | "But do not feel that families have to spend the whole visit there." |
| MAIN-0660 | 1280 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[22]/text()[1] | "The better approach is to mix a few attractions that genuinely excite the child with breaks, shows and family rides that keep the day sustainable." |

| MAIN-0662 | 1282 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[23]/text()[1] | "Waiting until everyone is exhausted usually means the break comes too late." |
| MAIN-0663 | 1283 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[24]/text()[1] | "Plan at least one period when nobody has to queue for a major attraction." |
| MAIN-0664 | 1284 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[25]/text()[1] | "Sit down, eat properly, use the restroom, reorganize bags and decide whether the original ride list still makes sense." |
| MAIN-0665 | 1285 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[26]/text()[1] | "If a child is already tired, adding another 70-minute queue because “it was on the plan” rarely improves the day." |
| MAIN-0666 | 1286 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[5]/text()[1] | "Strollers and baby facilities make the park easier — but they do not remove fatigue" |
| MAIN-0667 | 1287 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[27]/text()[1] | "Lotte World provides family facilities including stroller services, nursing or baby-care rooms, diaper-changing facilities and elevators." |
| ATTR-0016 | 1289 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Baby Care sign inside Lotte World Adventure" |
| MAIN-0668 | 1290 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0669 | 1292 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[28]/text()[1] | "These are useful, especially because Adventure covers multiple floors." |
| MAIN-0670 | 1293 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[29]/text()[1] | "They make logistics easier." |
| MAIN-0671 | 1294 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[30]/text()[1] | "They do not turn a full theme-park day into an effortless day for a small child." |
| MAIN-0672 | 1295 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[31]/text()[1] | "A stroller helps with walking. It does not help during every queue, ride restriction or crowded transition between areas." |
| MAIN-0673 | 1296 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[6]/text()[1] | "Parents may need different priorities" |
| MAIN-0674 | 1297 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[32]/text()[1] | "If adults want major thrill rides that younger children cannot use, decide in advance whether:" |
| MAIN-0675 | 1299 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[1]/text()[1] | "one adult waits with the child while another rides" |
| MAIN-0676 | 1300 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[2]/text()[1] | "the group skips the ride" |
| MAIN-0677 | 1301 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[3]/li[3]/text()[1] | "the adults split briefly and meet again at a clear location" |
| MAIN-0678 | 1303 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[33]/text()[1] | "Do not make this decision for the first time at the entrance to a 90-minute queue." |
| MAIN-0679 | 1304 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[34]/text()[1] | "The official park map includes a designated meeting area. Choosing a meeting point before the group separates is a simple precaution that can save unnecessary stress." |
| MAIN-0680 | 1305 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[7]/text()[1] | "Know when the ride plan is finished" |
| MAIN-0681 | 1306 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[35]/text()[1] | "You do not have to complete the original list." |
| MAIN-0682 | 1307 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[36]/text()[1] | "If the children have already had the rides they cared about, eaten, seen a show and enjoyed the park, another famous attraction does not automatically make the day better." |
| MAIN-0683 | 1308 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[37]/text()[1] | "This is especially important if you are also considering Seoul Sky, the Aquarium or a long Seokchon Lake walk afterward." |
| MAIN-0684 | 1309 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[38]/text()[1] | "For families, the best decision may be to stop while the day is still going well." |
| MAIN-0685 | 1310 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[39]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "A successful family day at Lotte World is not the maximum number of rides. It is getting the rides that mattered without exhausting everyone to reach them." |
| MAIN-0686 | 1318 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "STAY GUIDE" |
| MAIN-0687 | 1319 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Traveling with children? Your hotel location can make the rest of the Seoul trip much easier." |
| MAIN-0688 | 1320 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "A hotel near Lotte World can make the park day easier, but it is not automatically the best base for the rest of your Seoul trip." |
| MAIN-0689 | 1321 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Compare where to stay in Seoul" |
| MAIN-0690 | 1329 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Weather Can Change Your Ride Plan" |
| MAIN-0691 | 1332 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Lotte World has a large indoor park, but that does" |
| MAIN-0692 | 1332 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "not" |
| MAIN-0693 | 1332 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | "mean weather is irrelevant." |
| MAIN-0694 | 1333 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Several headline rides are on Magic Island, and outdoor attractions can suspend operation because of rain, strong wind, extreme temperatures or other on-site conditions." |
| ATTR-0017 | 1335 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Outdoor entrance to Lotte World Magic Island" |
| MAIN-0695 | 1336 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0696 | 1338 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "If Atlantis, Comet Express or another outdoor ride is one of your must-rides and the forecast looks unstable, consider protecting it earlier in the day rather than leaving it until the evening." |
| MAIN-0697 | 1339 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "If outdoor operations are interrupted, switch your attention to Adventure instead of spending hours waiting for Magic Island to reopen." |
| MAIN-0698 | 1340 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Bad weather does not ruin a Lotte World day — but it can ruin a plan that depends entirely on outdoor rides." |
| MAIN-0699 | 1348 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Food and Rest: Protect the Second Half of the Day" |
| MAIN-0700 | 1351 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Do not let every break become another queue." |
| MAIN-0701 | 1352 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If your morning has already included one or two long waits, use lunch or an afternoon break to actually reset. Sit down, eat properly, refill drinks and decide whether your original ride priorities still make sense." |
| ATTR-0018 | 1354 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Benches and shops inside Lotte World Adventure" |
| MAIN-0702 | 1355 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0703 | 1357 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "A famous restaurant or snack is not automatically worth another long line when the rest of your day is already under pressure." |
| MAIN-0704 | 1358 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "For most visitors, the better rule is simple:" |
| MAIN-0705 | 1359 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Queue for the rides that matter. Be flexible with food." |
| MAIN-0706 | 1360 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "With children, schedule a real break before everyone is exhausted. With adults, the same logic still applies — a 30-minute reset can protect the next three hours better than squeezing in one low-priority attraction." |
| MAIN-0707 | 1361 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Lotte World is easier to enjoy when meals and breaks are part of the plan, not what happens only after the plan has already failed." |
| MAIN-0708 | 1369 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Is the School Uniform Experience Worth the Time?" |
| MAIN-0709 | 1372 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Renting a Korean-style school uniform can be a fun part of a Lotte World visit, especially if photos and the K-culture experience matter to you." |
| MAIN-0710 | 1373 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "But it costs more than money. It also costs" |
| MAIN-0711 | 1373 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "time" |
| MAIN-0712 | 1373 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | "." |
| ATTR-0019 | 1375 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Visitors wearing Korean-style school uniforms at Lotte World" |
| MAIN-0713 | 1376 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0714 | 1378 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "You need to choose an outfit, change clothes, store your belongings and return the uniform later. On a day already dominated by ride queues, that can take time away from your must-rides." |
| MAIN-0715 | 1379 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "It makes sense if:" |
| MAIN-0716 | 1381 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "photos are one of the main reasons for your visit" |
| MAIN-0717 | 1382 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "you are visiting with friends or as a couple" |
| MAIN-0718 | 1383 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "you are happy to trade some ride time for the experience" |
| MAIN-0719 | 1385 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Skip it if:" |
| MAIN-0720 | 1387 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[1]/text()[1] | "you have several non-negotiable rides" |
| MAIN-0721 | 1388 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[2]/text()[1] | "you are on an After4 ticket" |
| MAIN-0722 | 1389 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[3]/text()[1] | "your schedule is already tight" |
| MAIN-0723 | 1390 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[2]/li[4]/text()[1] | "changing clothes and taking photos feels like another task rather than part of the fun" |
| MAIN-0724 | 1392 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Treat the uniform as an experience, not an automatic Lotte World requirement." |
| MAIN-0725 | 1400 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Getting to Lotte World" |
| MAIN-0726 | 1403 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Lotte World Adventure is directly connected to the Jamsil area and is easiest to reach by subway." |
| MAIN-0727 | 1404 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Take" |
| MAIN-0728 | 1404 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Subway Line 2 or Line 8 to Jamsil Station" |
| MAIN-0729 | 1404 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | ". For Lotte World Adventure, follow signs toward the" |
| MAIN-0730 | 1404 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "Exit 3 / Lotte World side" |

| MAIN-0735 | 1407 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "is the theme park at" |
| MAIN-0736 | 1407 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "240 Olympic-ro" |
| MAIN-0737 | 1407 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[2] | "." |
| MAIN-0738 | 1408 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[3]/text()[1] | "Lotte World Tower and Mall" |
| MAIN-0739 | 1408 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[4] | "are across the complex at" |
| MAIN-0740 | 1408 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[4]/text()[1] | "300 Olympic-ro" |
| MAIN-0741 | 1408 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[5] | "." |
| MAIN-0742 | 1409 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "If your ticket says Lotte World Adventure, follow the signs for the amusement park, not Seoul Sky or Lotte World Mall." |
| MAIN-0743 | 1410 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Arriving with luggage" |
| MAIN-0744 | 1411 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "If you are coming directly from the airport or changing hotels, avoid carrying large bags around the park if possible." |
| MAIN-0745 | 1412 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Luggage lockers are available inside the Lotte World complex, but sizes and availability can vary. If you have a large suitcase, check storage options before arriving rather than assuming every locker will fit it." |
| MAIN-0746 | 1413 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Best default:" |
| MAIN-0747 | 1413 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "arrive with only what you need for the day. Long queues, stairs and moving between Adventure and Magic Island are easier without travel luggage." |
| MAIN-0748 | 1421 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "STAY GUIDE" |
| MAIN-0749 | 1422 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[2]/div[1]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Still deciding where to stay in Seoul?" |
| MAIN-0750 | 1423 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Your hotel location affects airport access, luggage handling and how much subway time you spend getting around the city." |
| MAIN-0751 | 1424 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Find the right area to stay" |
| MAIN-0752 | 1432 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "What Not to Combine With a Full Lotte World Day" |
| MAIN-0753 | 1435 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Lotte World, the Aquarium, Seoul Sky and Seokchon Lake are all close together." |
| MAIN-0754 | 1436 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "That does" |
| MAIN-0755 | 1436 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "not" |
| MAIN-0756 | 1436 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | "mean they belong in the same day." |
| ATTR-0020 | 1438 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Seokchon Lake, Lotte World Magic Island and Lotte World Tower" |
| MAIN-0757 | 1439 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0758 | 1441 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "If rides are your priority, give Lotte World the time it needs. Long queues, meals, breaks and moving between Adventure and Magic Island can already fill most of the day." |
| MAIN-0759 | 1442 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Lotte World + Aquarium" |
| MAIN-0760 | 1443 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "This combination can work if Lotte World is only part of your day or if you are visiting with children who care more about variety than maximizing rides." |
| MAIN-0761 | 1444 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "It is a poor default for a full ride-focused day." |
| MAIN-0762 | 1445 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Lotte World + Seoul Sky" |
| MAIN-0763 | 1446 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "Possible, but only if you are comfortable leaving the park before you have exhausted your ride list." |
| MAIN-0764 | 1447 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Do not add Seoul Sky simply because it is next door." |
| MAIN-0765 | 1448 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Seokchon Lake" |
| MAIN-0766 | 1449 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "A short walk can be a pleasant break before or after Lotte World." |
| MAIN-0767 | 1450 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "A full lake walk is a different activity and needs its own time." |
| MAIN-0768 | 1451 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[4]/text()[1] | "The better rule" |
| MAIN-0769 | 1452 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[10]/text()[1] | "If Lotte World is the main reason you came to Jamsil:" |
| MAIN-0770 | 1453 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[11]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Give it the day. Add something else only if your Lotte World plan finishes early." |
| MAIN-0771 | 1454 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[12]/text()[1] | "Close together does not mean they belong in the same itinerary." |

| MAIN-0776 | 1467 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | ", with larger discounts sometimes available during promotions." |
| MAIN-0777 | 1468 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Prices change by date and offer, so compare the current rate before booking." |
| MAIN-0778 | 1469 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Before you pay, check:" |
