| MAIN-0783 | 1474 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "cancellation and refund conditions" |
| MAIN-0784 | 1475 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/ul[1]/li[5]/text()[1] | "whether the ticket is admission only or part of a package" |
| MAIN-0785 | 1477 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Magic Pass is separate from normal admission unless the product specifically says otherwise." |
| MAIN-0786 | 1478 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Check the current Lotte World ticket price" |
| MAIN-0787 | 1479 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "If you already know your visit date, check the current Trip.com price against Lotte World's regular price before buying." |
| MAIN-0788 | 1489 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/a[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check Lotte World Adventure tickets on Trip.com" |
| MAIN-0789 | 1490 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "Prices and promotions can change, so the price shown on the booking page at the time of purchase is the one that matters." |
| MAIN-0790 | 1499 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Lotte World Seoul FAQ" |
| MAIN-0791 | 1503 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Is one day enough for Lotte World Seoul?" |
| MAIN-0792 | 1504 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes, for most visitors one full day is enough to have a good Lotte World experience, but not necessarily enough to ride everything. Long queues can quickly reduce how many major attractions you complete, so choose your 3–5 must-rides first." |
| MAIN-0793 | 1507 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Is After4 worth it?" |
| MAIN-0794 | 1508 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "After4 can be good value if you want a shorter evening visit, only have a few ride priorities or care as much about the atmosphere as the number of rides." |
| MAIN-0795 | 1509 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If this is your only visit and several headline rides are non-negotiable, a full-day ticket is usually the safer choice." |
| MAIN-0796 | 1512 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Is Magic Pass worth it?" |
| MAIN-0797 | 1513 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/p[1]/text()[1] | "It depends on your must-rides, the expected queues and how much you value your time." |
| MAIN-0798 | 1514 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Magic Pass makes more sense when several rides you genuinely care about are eligible and likely to have long waits. It is much less useful if your priorities are flexible or younger children cannot use many of the covered rides." |
| MAIN-0799 | 1517 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Can Magic Pass sell out?" |
| MAIN-0800 | 1518 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes. Magic Pass is sold in limited quantities and can sell out." |
| MAIN-0801 | 1519 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If it is important to your plan, check the current official purchase rules before your visit rather than assuming you can buy it later in the day." |
| MAIN-0802 | 1522 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Is Lotte World good for young children?" |
| MAIN-0803 | 1523 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes, but plan differently from a thrill-ride visit." |
| MAIN-0804 | 1524 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Check height restrictions first, use the family and children's attractions that fit your group, and build in real breaks. For younger children, managing energy usually matters more than maximizing ride count." |
| MAIN-0805 | 1527 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Is Lotte World mostly indoors?" |
| MAIN-0806 | 1528 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Adventure is a large indoor theme park, but Magic Island is outdoors and includes several major thrill rides." |
| MAIN-0807 | 1529 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Rain, wind or extreme temperatures can therefore still affect your visit, especially if your must-rides are on Magic Island." |
| MAIN-0808 | 1532 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[7]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "What are the best rides at Lotte World?" |
| MAIN-0809 | 1533 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[7]/p[1]/text()[1] | "There is no single best list for everyone." |
| MAIN-0810 | 1534 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[7]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Thrill-focused visitors often prioritize rides such as Atlantis, Comet Express, French Revolution and the Gyro attractions, while families may choose very differently after checking height restrictions." |
| MAIN-0811 | 1535 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[7]/p[3]/text()[1] | "The better question is which 3–5 rides would make your own visit feel successful." |
| MAIN-0812 | 1538 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[8]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Can I visit Lotte World, Seoul Sky and the Aquarium on the same day?" |
| MAIN-0813 | 1539 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[8]/p[1]/text()[1] | "You can, but it is usually too much for a ride-focused Lotte World day." |
| MAIN-0814 | 1540 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/details[8]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If Lotte World is your main reason for visiting Jamsil, give it the day and add Seoul Sky or the Aquarium only if you intentionally want a shorter theme-park visit." |
| MAIN-0815 | 1549 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "STAY GUIDE" |
| MAIN-0816 | 1550 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[3]/div[1]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Planning the rest of your Seoul stay?" |
| MAIN-0817 | 1551 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Where you sleep can change airport access, daily subway time, luggage handling and how easy it is to get back after a long day." |

| MAIN-0003 | 91 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Korea is one of the easiest places to explore skincare, makeup, personal color, hair care and beauty treatments in a single trip. Seoul has everything from large Olive Young stores and independent fragrance shops to appointment-only salons and clinics." |
| MAIN-0004 | 92 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "K-Beauty days tend to split naturally in two: products you can browse whenever you have time, and services worth putting on the calendar before the trip. If something becomes a favorite, save the exact product name, shade and size while the packaging is still in front of you." |
| ATTR-0001 | 95 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Traveler discussing skincare products with a consultant at a K-Beauty counter" |
| MAIN-0005 | 102 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "K-Beauty is more than a shopping stop" |
| MAIN-0006 | 103 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Retail shopping can be spontaneous, while color analysis, salon visits, and clinic consultations usually need more time and planning." |
| ATTR-0002 | 106 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/img[1]/@alt | "K-Beauty store consultant helping a traveler test a skincare serum" |
| MAIN-0007 | 107 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Skincare Shopping" |
| MAIN-0008 | 107 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Skincare is the easiest part of K-Beauty to explore without much planning. Olive Young branches are everywhere in Seoul, while department stores, brand flagships and smaller specialty shops offer a wider range of products and price points." |
| MAIN-0009 | 107 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If you are trying a product for the first time, especially on sensitive skin, avoid opening several new products at once. It is also worth photographing the exact product name and size before leaving Korea, because packaging can look very similar across a brand's range." |
| ATTR-0003 | 111 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Shopper opening a lipstick tester beside makeup brushes and color products" |
| MAIN-0010 | 113 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Makeup & Personal Color" |
| MAIN-0011 | 113 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Makeup shopping can be as simple as testing lip colors in a store, but personal color consultations usually require more planning. Popular studios often work by appointment, and English availability can vary by location and time." |
| MAIN-0012 | 113 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "A consultation is most useful when you want help narrowing down shades rather than simply buying whatever is trending. Before booking, check the session length, language support and what is actually included in the price." |
| MAIN-0013 | 116 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Hair & Scalp Care" |
| MAIN-0014 | 116 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Hair salons and scalp treatments are easy to add to a Seoul itinerary, particularly in Hongdae, Gangnam and other busy shopping districts. Some travelers come for a haircut or styling, while others book a scalp analysis or treatment that is less common at home." |
| MAIN-0015 | 116 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For anything more involved than a basic cut or treatment, send reference photos in advance and confirm the expected duration. Popular salons can fill up quickly, especially on weekends." |
| ATTR-0004 | 119 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Rolled spa towel and botanical treatment oils prepared for a skincare service" |
| MAIN-0016 | 120 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Beauty Clinics & Consultations" |
| MAIN-0017 | 120 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul has a large number of dermatology and aesthetic clinics, but this is the part of K-Beauty that deserves the most careful planning. Prices, treatment recommendations, English support and follow-up policies can differ significantly between clinics." |
| MAIN-0018 | 120 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Before agreeing to a procedure, confirm the total cost, possible side effects, recovery time, aftercare instructions and who will perform the treatment. A consultation should help you understand the options; it should not be treated as a guarantee of a particular result." |
| MAIN-0019 | 123 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Fragrance & Lifestyle Beauty" |
| MAIN-0020 | 123 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "K-Beauty is no longer limited to skincare and makeup. Seoul also has independent fragrance houses, body-care brands and lifestyle beauty stores that are especially easy to explore in areas such as Seongsu and Hannam." |
| MAIN-0021 | 123 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Fragrance shopping is better done slowly. After testing several scents, take a break before deciding, because scent fatigue can make everything start to smell similar." |
| MAIN-0022 | 131 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Where to explore K-Beauty in Seoul" |
| MAIN-0023 | 132 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "The useful part of Seoul changes with what you are actually doing. Myeongdong keeps broad shopping compact, Seongsu is more interesting when discovering newer brands is part of the day, and southern Seoul becomes more practical when several appointments are already booked." |
| MAIN-0024 | 136 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/caption[1]/text()[1] | "Comparison of Seoul K-Beauty areas" |

| MAIN-0027 | 137 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/thead[1]/tr[1]/th[3]/text()[1] | "Planning needed" |

| MAIN-0031 | 139 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[1]/td[2]/text()[1] | "Usually little planning for retail shopping" |

| MAIN-0035 | 140 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[2]/td[2]/text()[1] | "Check current pop-ups or specific brand events shortly before visiting" |

| MAIN-0037 | 141 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[3]/th[1]/text()[1] | "Hongdae" |
| MAIN-0038 | 141 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[3]/td[1]/text()[1] | "Beauty shopping combined with salons, cafés and nightlife" |
| MAIN-0039 | 141 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[3]/td[2]/text()[1] | "Shopping is easy to add spontaneously; salon visits may need appointments" |
| MAIN-0040 | 141 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[3]/td[3]/text()[1] | "Less concentrated than Myeongdong for large-scale skincare comparison" |

| MAIN-0043 | 142 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[4]/td[2]/text()[1] | "Book specialist services in advance and allow time between locations" |
| MAIN-0044 | 142 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[4]/td[3]/text()[1] | "Appointments can be spread across southern Seoul" |

| MAIN-0048 | 143 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/table[1]/tbody[1]/tr[5]/td[3]/text()[1] | "Higher prices and fewer reliable walk-ins" |
| ATTR-0005 | 149 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[1]/div[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Busy OLIVE YOUNG storefront on a Seoul shopping street" |
| MAIN-0049 | 149 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[1]/div[2]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Myeongdong" |
| MAIN-0050 | 149 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[1]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Myeongdong is the easiest starting point for first-time K-Beauty shopping. Large Olive Young stores, road-shop brands, department stores and duty-free shopping are concentrated within a compact area, so it is possible to compare many products without spending much time on transportation." |
| MAIN-0051 | 149 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[1]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The trade-off is that the area is busy and strongly oriented toward visitors. If your main goal is efficient skincare and makeup shopping, that convenience is usually more important than the lack of a local neighborhood feel." |
| MAIN-0052 | 150 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[2]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Seongsu" |
| MAIN-0053 | 150 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[2]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seongsu is better for travelers interested in newer Korean brands, concept stores, pop-ups and fragrance rather than simply filling a shopping bag as quickly as possible. Beauty stops fit naturally into a day of cafés, fashion and design shops." |
| MAIN-0054 | 150 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[2]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Stores and pop-ups change frequently, so a specific brand or event should be checked shortly before visiting rather than assumed to be permanent." |
| MAIN-0055 | 151 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[3]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Hongdae" |
| MAIN-0056 | 151 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[3]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Hongdae works well when K-Beauty is only one part of a busier day. Makeup stores, hair salons, cafés, nightlife and casual shopping are easy to combine, and many businesses stay active into the evening." |
| MAIN-0057 | 151 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[3]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "It is especially convenient for younger travelers and anyone already staying around Hongdae, although it is less concentrated than Myeongdong for large-scale skincare shopping." |
| MAIN-0058 | 152 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[4]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Gangnam" |
| MAIN-0059 | 152 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[4]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Gangnam is one of the most practical areas for appointment-based beauty services. Hair salons, personal color studios, dermatology clinics and other specialist services are spread across Gangnam, Sinsa and nearby districts." |
| MAIN-0060 | 152 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[4]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Distances can be greater than they first appear on a map, so appointments should be grouped carefully rather than booked back-to-back across different parts of southern Seoul." |
| MAIN-0061 | 153 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[5]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Apgujeong & Cheongdam" |
| MAIN-0062 | 153 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[5]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Apgujeong and Cheongdam are better suited to travelers looking for premium salons, clinics and higher-end beauty experiences. Many visits here are planned around a specific appointment rather than casual browsing." |
| MAIN-0063 | 153 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[2]/article[5]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Prices are generally higher, and spontaneous walk-ins are less reliable than in major shopping districts. Confirm the service, price and appointment details before making a special trip." |
| MAIN-0064 | 161 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Buy in Korea or reorder later?" |
| MAIN-0065 | 162 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Buying in Korea gives you the widest choice and lets you check the product, shade and packaging in person. It also avoids international shipping, but anything you buy has to fit into your luggage and comply with airline restrictions." |
| MAIN-0066 | 163 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Buying again at home is usually easier for products that have an official local distributor. The price may be higher, but returns, delivery and customer service are generally simpler." |
| MAIN-0067 | 164 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "If you think you may want the same product again, save the exact English or Korean name, shade number, size and a clear photo of the packaging before you leave. International ordering can fill the gaps when a product is not sold locally, but shipping, taxes and return rules can erase what looked like a cheaper price, so compare the final delivered cost rather than the product price alone." |
| MAIN-0068 | 167 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Olive Young operates different online stores depending on the delivery destination." |
| MAIN-0069 | 168 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Travelers shipping to the United States should use OLIVE YOUNG US, while other supported destinations are generally served through OLIVE YOUNG Global. Availability, shipping fees and promotions can differ between the two stores." |

| MAIN-0075 | 187 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/aside[1]/div[2]/div[2]/div[1]/article[2]/h4[1]/text()[1] | "OLIVE YOUNG Global" |

| MAIN-0077 | 189 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/aside[1]/div[2]/div[2]/div[1]/article[2]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Visit OLIVE YOUNG Global" |

| MAIN-0079 | 201 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Common K-Beauty planning questions" |
| MAIN-0080 | 203 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Where should first-time visitors shop?" |
| MAIN-0081 | 203 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Myeongdong is the easiest place to compare a lot of skincare and makeup in one area, and the larger stores make it simple to see several brands side by side. The downside is that promotions and bundled sets can turn a quick stop into a much bigger purchase than expected." |
| MAIN-0082 | 204 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Do beauty clinics require reservations?" |
| MAIN-0083 | 204 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Many consultations and procedures do, especially when an interpreter or specific clinician is requested. The appointment, consultation process, total price, risks and aftercare should be confirmed directly with the provider." |
| MAIN-0084 | 205 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Is personal color analysis available in English?" |
| MAIN-0085 | 205 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Some studios offer English sessions or interpretation, but availability varies. The working language, session length, report format and cancellation policy should be clear before booking." |
| MAIN-0086 | 206 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Are products cheaper in Korea?" |
| MAIN-0087 | 206 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Some products may have favorable local pricing or sets, while others do not. The exact size and formula matter, along with luggage, shipping, tax and return limitations." |
| MAIN-0088 | 207 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Can I reorder products after returning home?" |
| MAIN-0089 | 207 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes. Many K-beauty products can be reordered after you return home. Shoppers in the United States can use OLIVE YOUNG US, while travelers in other supported countries can order through OLIVE YOUNG Global. Product availability and shipping eligibility may vary by destination." |
| MAIN-0090 | 208 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "How should I compare official stores and marketplaces?" |
| MAIN-0091 | 208 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seller identity, product origin, return terms and whether the manufacturer recognizes the sales channel are all important. A lower price is less useful when authenticity or support is unclear." |
| MAIN-0092 | 209 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[7]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "What should sensitive-skin travelers check?" |
| MAIN-0093 | 209 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[7]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Ingredients and instructions deserve careful review, with a patch test where appropriate and no introduction of several unfamiliar products together. Qualified medical advice is important for active conditions or previous serious reactions." |
| MAIN-0094 | 210 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[8]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Which Seoul area is best for a K-Beauty-focused trip?" |
| MAIN-0095 | 210 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/details[8]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Myeongdong favors easy retail, Seongsu favors trends, Hongdae combines casual shopping with nightlife, and Gangnam or Apgujeong suit scheduled premium services. A nearby hotel is most useful only when the area supports several plans." |
| MAIN-0096 | 218 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Where should K-Beauty travelers stay in Seoul?" |
| MAIN-0097 | 219 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "If K-Beauty is only part of the trip, there is usually no reason to move hotels for it. A central base is easier when shops and appointments are scattered across Seoul; staying near one beauty-heavy area makes more sense only when several bookings are concentrated there." |
| MAIN-0098 | 220 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Think of the hotel as part of the daily route, not as a way to stay inside one “K-Beauty district.”" |

|---|---:|---|---|
| HEAD-0001 | 16 | /html[1]/head[1]/title[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Sky Guide 2026" |
| SCHEMA-0001 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/0/name | "Is Seoul Sky the same as Lotte World Tower?" |
| SCHEMA-0002 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/0/acceptedAnswer/text | "Seoul Sky is the paid observatory inside Lotte World Tower. The tower and mall are the wider complex; Lotte World Adventure is the separate amusement park. Check the attraction name on the ticket you buy." |
| SCHEMA-0003 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/1/name | "How long should I allow for Seoul Sky?" |
| SCHEMA-0004 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/1/acceptedAnswer/text | "We suggest around 90–120 minutes for an ordinary observatory visit, with travel and substantial queues on top. This is a planning allowance, not an official duration. Allow longer for the change from daylight to night, a meal or a Sky Bridge booking." |
| SCHEMA-0005 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/2/name | "Is sunset always the best time to go?" |
| SCHEMA-0006 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/2/acceptedAnswer/text | "No. Choose sunset for the change from daylight to the evening skyline. Clear daylight can be a better choice when seeing the city in detail matters more. Let the conditions and your schedule guide the decision." |
| SCHEMA-0007 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/3/name | "Do I need Fast Pass?" |
| SCHEMA-0008 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/3/acceptedAnswer/text | "Not automatically. General admission is our starting choice for ordinary viewing. Consider Fast Pass when priority entry would solve a real waiting problem for your group, and read the selected ticket's benefits before paying more." |
| SCHEMA-0009 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/4/name | "Does a QR ticket let me skip every queue?" |
| SCHEMA-0010 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/4/acceptedAnswer/text | "No. QR is a ticket or voucher format, not proof of priority admission. Direct-entry instructions may let you bypass ticket exchange; that is different from Fast Pass. Follow the instructions attached to your booking." |
| SCHEMA-0011 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/5/name | "What if every date looks sold out online?" |
| SCHEMA-0012 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/5/acceptedAnswer/text | "Check the selected product and booking period, then ask Seoul Sky or the seller about the date you need. A calendar showing no availability is not enough to establish whether other ticket options are available. Do not assume either guaranteed entry or a website error." |
| SCHEMA-0013 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/6/name | "Can I combine Seoul Sky and the Aquarium?" |
| SCHEMA-0014 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/6/acceptedAnswer/text | "Yes, when you want both visits and have left time for each. Confirm the package's valid dates and visiting order before booking. We would not add a full Lotte World Adventure day to that plan by default." |
| SCHEMA-0015 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/7/name | "Do I need to stay in Jamsil to visit Seoul Sky?" |
| SCHEMA-0016 | 210 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/7/acceptedAnswer/text | "No. Choose your hotel around the whole Seoul trip. Staying in Jamsil is a separate decision based on how often you expect to use the area, not a requirement for one observatory visit." |
