| MAIN-0006 | 343 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Tickets, timing and the view from Lotte World Tower" |
| ATTR-0001 | 349 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/picture[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Lotte World Tower beside Seokchon Lake in Jamsil, Seoul" |
| MAIN-0007 | 351 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Photo: Seoul Tourism Organization" |
| MAIN-0008 | 358 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Sky is the paid observatory in Lotte World Tower. For a first visit focused on the view, our starting choice is" |
| MAIN-0009 | 358 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "general admission on a day with good visibility" |
| MAIN-0010 | 358 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | "." |
| MAIN-0011 | 359 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Choose sunset when you want to watch daylight turn into the evening skyline. A clear daytime visit is also worth considering; there is no need to build your entire day around sunset if the weather or your schedule works against it." |
| MAIN-0012 | 360 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Fast Pass is a separate ticket choice to consider when waiting would be a problem. It cannot improve a hazy view. Sky Bridge is a different outdoor experience, so decide whether you want that activity before comparing its price with an ordinary observatory ticket." |
| MAIN-0013 | 361 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "You can visit Seoul Sky as one part of a Jamsil day. Leave room for finding the entrance, admission, looking around and getting back down, rather than treating it as a quick stop between two tightly timed bookings." |
| MAIN-0014 | 368 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Is Seoul Sky Worth Visiting?" |
| MAIN-0015 | 371 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "The appeal is seeing the scale of Seoul. On a clear day, the river and the spread of the city become part of the experience, rather than a backdrop to one photograph. Give yourself time to look around instead of heading straight for a photo spot and leaving." |
| MAIN-0016 | 372 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "This is a stronger reason to go than simply wanting to tick off another landmark. A couple planning an evening around the view may get more from the visit than a group squeezing it in after a long day at the amusement park." |
| MAIN-0017 | 373 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Already spending time in Jamsil? You can fit the observatory around a meal or a shorter nearby activity. Coming only for Seoul Sky? Include the journey from your hotel when deciding how much of the day to give it." |
| ATTR-0002 | 375 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Visitor looking over Seoul from inside the Seoul Sky observatory" |
| MAIN-0018 | 376 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "The observatory experience depends as much on visibility as on height — check the conditions before building the day around the view." |
| MAIN-0019 | 376 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[2] | "Photo: Evgeny Matveev / Unsplash" |
| MAIN-0020 | 378 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "When to leave it out" |
| MAIN-0021 | 379 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "If your Seoul trip is short and a high-rise view is not particularly important to you, spend the time on something you would be more disappointed to miss." |
| MAIN-0022 | 380 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Be equally honest about poor visibility. If distant city views are the reason you are paying, heavy haze or low cloud may be a reason to change days. You may still enjoy the height and the indoor experience, but that is a different expectation from seeing a clear skyline." |
| MAIN-0023 | 381 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "With children, consider how the visit fits the rest of the day. A short look at the city may be enough. Waiting for sunset after everyone has already had a full day of rides is a bigger commitment." |
| MAIN-0024 | 389 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Best Time to Visit Seoul Sky: Daylight, Sunset or Night?" |
| MAIN-0025 | 392 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Start with the view you want, then check the conditions for your date. There is no single clock time that works throughout the year." |
| MAIN-0026 | 393 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Daylight: for seeing the city itself" |
| MAIN-0027 | 394 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Choose daylight if you want to make out the river, the city layout and the surrounding landscape. Visibility matters more for this kind of visit than getting a particular photograph at a fashionable hour." |
| MAIN-0028 | 395 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "There is no need to stay until evening to justify the admission price. A clear daytime visit can be a complete experience, leaving the rest of the day for a meal, the lake or another part of Seoul." |
| ATTR-0003 | 398 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Han River and eastern Seoul viewed from Lotte World Tower" |
| MAIN-0029 | 399 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "A clear daytime visit makes the Han River, bridges and surrounding districts easier to read from above." |
| MAIN-0030 | 399 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[2] | "Photo:" |
| MAIN-0031 | 399 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Tristan Surtel / Wikimedia Commons" |
| MAIN-0032 | 399 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[3] | "·" |
| MAIN-0033 | 399 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/a[2]/text()[1] | "CC BY-SA 4.0" |
| MAIN-0034 | 399 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[4] | "· resized" |
| MAIN-0035 | 401 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Sunset: for the change from day to night" |
| MAIN-0036 | 402 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Sunset is the choice for people who want to watch the scene change. That means allowing time to arrive while it is still light and staying long enough for the evening view." |
| MAIN-0037 | 403 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Plan to be on the viewing floors before sunset, not just arriving at Jamsil Station then." |
| MAIN-0038 | 403 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Finding the entrance, checking your ticket, any required exchange and the admission queue all come before the view." |
| MAIN-0039 | 404 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "Look up sunset for your actual visit date and work backward. Buying online does not, by itself, guarantee that you will reach the viewing floors at a particular minute." |
| MAIN-0040 | 405 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Keep your next reservation loose enough to allow for the visit. A tightly booked dinner immediately afterward can leave you choosing between the evening view and arriving on time." |
| MAIN-0041 | 406 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Night: for the lights, without the sunset wait" |
| MAIN-0042 | 407 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "An evening visit can suit a day when you are busy elsewhere and mainly want the illuminated city. You do not need to arrive in daylight simply because other visitors prefer to see both." |
| MAIN-0043 | 408 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "Expect a different experience from looking across the city during the day. Choose night for the lights rather than expecting the same landscape detail after dark." |
| MAIN-0044 | 409 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/p[10]/text()[1] | "Check the admission cutoff for your visit date as well as closing time. Leave enough time to enjoy the view before you need to come back down." |
| MAIN-0045 | 417 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Clouds, Haze and Rain: Should You Change the Plan?" |
| MAIN-0046 | 420 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Sky's indoor setting does not guarantee a clear view. Rain, low cloud and haze can change what you can see, even when the observatory is admitting visitors." |
| MAIN-0047 | 421 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For a view-focused visit, check current conditions before committing to a ticket you cannot change. Consider cloud, haze and rain together; one favorable weather or air-quality reading is not a promise of a clear skyline." |
| MAIN-0048 | 422 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "A rainy forecast does not have to end the plan. Ask whether you would still enjoy the visit if distant views were limited. If the answer is no and you have another day available, moving the visit is a reasonable choice." |
| MAIN-0049 | 423 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Let the ticket conditions match that flexibility" |
| MAIN-0050 | 424 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "A flexible itinerary is only useful if the ticket allows the change you have in mind." |
| MAIN-0051 | 425 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Before paying, check whether the ticket is valid on one selected day or within a stated period. Then check cancellation and date-change conditions separately. A ticket with a longer validity period is not necessarily refundable." |
| MAIN-0052 | 426 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "If you have already booked, read the conditions before abandoning the visit or buying a replacement. Poor visibility should not be treated as a guaranteed right to a refund." |
| MAIN-0053 | 427 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Fast Pass addresses a different question: how much waiting you are prepared to accept. Decide whether today's view is worth the visit before paying extra for priority admission." |
| MAIN-0054 | 435 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "How Much Time Should You Allow?" |
| MAIN-0055 | 438 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "For an ordinary observatory visit," |
| MAIN-0056 | 438 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "we suggest setting aside around 90–120 minutes, with travel and any substantial queues on top" |
| MAIN-0057 | 438 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | ". This is a planning allowance, not an official visit duration or a guarantee of when you will be back outside." |
| MAIN-0058 | 439 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Watching daylight turn into night takes as long as that change takes. A meal or a Sky Bridge booking needs its own allowance too. Do not fit those into the same two-hour block by assuming the rest of the visit will be quick." |
| MAIN-0059 | 440 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Think through the whole sequence: reaching the entrance, sorting out the ticket, going up, looking around and coming back down. The elevator ride alone tells you very little about how much time to leave between arrival and your next appointment." |
| MAIN-0060 | 441 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Chosen your visit day? Compare the admission options, then check the ticket's valid date and cancellation terms." |
| MAIN-0061 | 452 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[1]/a[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check Seoul Sky tickets for your date" |
| MAIN-0062 | 461 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Which Seoul Sky Ticket Should You Buy?" |
| MAIN-0063 | 464 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "For a visit focused on the view," |
| MAIN-0064 | 464 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "general admission is our starting recommendation" |
| MAIN-0065 | 464 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | ". Consider Fast Pass when the admission queue could disrupt your plans. Sky Bridge is a separate outdoor experience, so choose it because you want that activity rather than treating it as the next level of an ordinary ticket." |
| MAIN-0066 | 465 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "General admission: start here for an ordinary observatory visit" |
| MAIN-0067 | 466 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "A regular ticket is the straightforward choice when you want to look around, take photographs and spend time with the view. You do not need to upgrade simply because this is your only visit to Seoul Sky." |
| MAIN-0068 | 467 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "When comparing prices, make sure the product names" |
| MAIN-0069 | 467 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Sky" |
| MAIN-0070 | 467 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[2] | ", not just Lotte World. The amusement park, observatory and Aquarium are different attractions. A ticket for one should not be treated as admission to the others unless the package explicitly includes them." |
| MAIN-0071 | 468 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Fast Pass: consider what a long wait would cost you" |
| MAIN-0072 | 469 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Fast Pass is an admission ticket offering priority entry. Do not automatically buy general admission first and then purchase Fast Pass as though it must be an add-on. Check the inclusions before paying twice for access to the observatory." |
| MAIN-0073 | 470 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "We would consider it when your visit time is fixed or someone in your group would find a long wait difficult. With a flexible afternoon, the extra cost is harder to justify without knowing how busy admission is likely to be. For a family, compare the total for everyone who needs the ticket, not just the difference for one adult." |
| MAIN-0074 | 471 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "Read what the priority benefit covers. Do not assume it removes every queue during the visit or guarantees the exact minute you will reach the viewing floors. Leave some room before your next reservation even when buying priority entry." |
| MAIN-0075 | 472 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Sky Bridge and combination tickets" |
| MAIN-0076 | 473 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Sky Bridge is a separately booked outdoor tour with its own participation conditions. Before buying an additional observatory ticket, check what admission is included in the tour you are considering." |
| MAIN-0077 | 474 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "An Aquarium combination is worth comparing when both attractions are already part of your plan. Check each admission's valid date and whether the package requires a particular visiting order. Do not add an attraction you were not interested in just to use a discount." |
| MAIN-0078 | 485 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[1]/a[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Compare Seoul Sky ticket options for your date" |
| MAIN-0079 | 494 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "A QR Ticket Is Not the Same as Fast Pass" |
| MAIN-0080 | 497 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "A QR code tells you how a ticket or voucher is presented. It does not, by itself, tell you which queue you can skip." |
| MAIN-0081 | 498 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "With a" |
| MAIN-0082 | 498 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "direct-entry ticket" |
| MAIN-0083 | 498 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | ", the instructions may let you show the QR code at the entrance without visiting the ticket counter. A" |
| MAIN-0084 | 498 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "voucher requiring exchange" |
| MAIN-0085 | 498 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[3] | "sends you to the counter first, even if that voucher also contains a QR code. Both types appear in Seoul Sky ticket listings." |
| MAIN-0086 | 499 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Neither format alone proves that you have priority admission. Look for Fast Pass or an explicit priority-entry benefit in the selected option, rather than relying on a photograph of a QR code or a general “skip the line” headline." |
| MAIN-0087 | 500 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Before joining a queue, open the redemption instructions on your actual booking. Showing staff the voucher is more useful than showing only the payment receipt. Keep any identification requested by the seller available as well." |
| MAIN-0088 | 508 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Buying Online: Check When You Can Actually Use the Ticket" |
| MAIN-0089 | 511 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "An online purchase can save a ticket-counter stop, but only when the selected product allows direct entry. Confirmation time, validity and cancellation are separate conditions; buying online does not make all three flexible." |
| MAIN-0090 | 512 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Visiting today? Check confirmation first" |
| MAIN-0091 | 513 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Some Seoul Sky products offer instant confirmation, while others allow up to 24 hours to confirm and issue the voucher. That difference matters when you are already in Jamsil or hoping to visit around sunset." |
| MAIN-0092 | 514 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "For a same-day visit, check that the ticket can be confirmed and used in time. An order confirmation email is not necessarily the admission voucher. Wait for the usable ticket and read any exchange instructions before treating the booking as ready for entry." |
| MAIN-0093 | 515 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Check what the selected date means" |
| MAIN-0094 | 516 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "A ticket valid" |
| MAIN-0095 | 516 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "on" |
| MAIN-0096 | 516 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[2] | "one date is different from a ticket valid" |
| MAIN-0097 | 516 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "until" |
| MAIN-0098 | 516 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[3] | "a stated date. Read the wording attached to the exact option you are buying; a calendar selection alone does not explain its rules." |
| MAIN-0099 | 517 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Likewise, a longer use period does not mean free cancellation. Confirm the last usable date, any date-change conditions and the refund policy before relying on the ticket for a weather-dependent plan." |
| MAIN-0100 | 518 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "Check who can use the offer too. Some discounted products are restricted to foreign visitors, and the seller may ask for a passport. A lower advertised price is only relevant if everyone in your booking meets the conditions." |
| MAIN-0101 | 519 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[3]/text()[1] | "Compare the usable ticket, not just the headline price" |
| MAIN-0102 | 520 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "Once two options cover the same admission, compare the amount payable for your whole group alongside confirmation and redemption. Saving a little may not be worthwhile if the cheaper option requires a counter exchange or cannot be issued in time for your visit." |
| MAIN-0103 | 521 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "You do not need the most flexible ticket for every trip. You need conditions you can actually meet." |
| MAIN-0104 | 532 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[1]/a[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check Seoul Sky admission prices and booking conditions" |
| MAIN-0105 | 541 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Every Date Looks Sold Out Online. What Should You Do?" |
| MAIN-0106 | 544 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Do not immediately abandon the visit—or assume that the booking page is wrong. Travelers have reported calendars showing no availability across multiple dates, followed by successful purchases at the venue. Those reports are not a guarantee of tickets on your date." |
| MAIN-0107 | 545 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "First, check the product you selected. Ordinary admission, Fast Pass and a scheduled Sky Bridge tour are different bookings. An unavailable option should not be treated as proof that every way of visiting is sold out." |
| MAIN-0108 | 546 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Then check whether your date is within the displayed booking period. If the message still appears across every date you try, contact Seoul Sky or the seller before making a special journey. Ask specifically about the ticket type and day you need." |
| MAIN-0109 | 547 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Another seller's listing is only an alternative if its selected option is available, valid for your visit and able to issue the ticket in time. Avoid buying an unwanted upgrade simply because it is the first option that appears available." |
| MAIN-0110 | 548 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Already in Jamsil? Ask at the Seoul Sky ticket counter before assuming that online availability tells the whole story. Keep the rest of the day flexible until admission is confirmed." |
| MAIN-0111 | 556 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Finding the Seoul Sky Entrance" |
| MAIN-0112 | 559 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Head for" |
| MAIN-0113 | 559 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "SEOUL SKY on B1 of the Lotte World Tower/Mall complex" |
| MAIN-0114 | 559 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | ", at 300 Olympic-ro. Finding the tower is only the first step; you still need the observatory entrance." |
| MAIN-0115 | 560 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "At Jamsil Station, follow the signs toward the Tower/Mall complex and look specifically for" |
| MAIN-0116 | 560 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "SEOUL SKY" |
| MAIN-0117 | 560 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | ". Lotte World Adventure is the amusement park, so its entrance is not where you redeem an ordinary Seoul Sky ticket. Keeping the Korean name," |
| MAIN-0118 | 560 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[2]/text()[1] | "서울스카이" |
| MAIN-0119 | 560 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[3] | ", on your phone gives you something specific to show when asking for directions." |
| MAIN-0120 | 561 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "The information desk is beside the ticket booth in the B1 lobby. If you are unsure where your booking should be used, show staff the ticket and its redemption instructions before joining a line. This is also the place to ask for a floor guide rather than trying to navigate from an old screenshot." |
| MAIN-0121 | 569 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "What Happens Once You Arrive?" |
| MAIN-0122 | 572 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Sort out any required ticket exchange first, then follow the admission route indicated by staff. Keep the usable ticket available until you have passed the relevant checks." |
| MAIN-0123 | 573 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Exhibitions and media content are part of the visit, and the" |
| MAIN-0124 | 573 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Sky Shuttle" |
| MAIN-0125 | 573 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[2] | "elevator uses screens during the ascent. The ride up is an introduction to the observatory; it does not account for the time spent reaching the entrance, entering and getting around the viewing areas." |
| MAIN-0126 | 574 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Once upstairs, give yourself a first look at the city before moving straight to a photograph you have seen online. We would spend time with the panorama first, then decide how much of the visit to give to the glass floor, an outdoor stop or food and drinks." |
| MAIN-0127 | 575 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "When you are ready to leave, allow time to follow the exit route and return to ground level. A restaurant reservation downstairs needs more than the time it takes to put your camera away." |
| MAIN-0128 | 583 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Sky Deck and Sky Terrace: Know the Difference" |
| MAIN-0129 | 586 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Sky Deck: the glass-floor experience" |
| MAIN-0130 | 587 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Sky Deck is the glass-floor viewing area." |
| MAIN-0131 | 587 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Its appeal is the view beneath your feet, which is a different experience from looking across the skyline through a window." |
| MAIN-0132 | 588 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Decide whether that sensation is something you actually enjoy. Someone who loves high views may still dislike looking straight down through glass. There is no need to make a glass-floor photograph the measure of a successful visit." |
| MAIN-0133 | 589 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "With children, let curiosity set the pace. A child who is happy looking out at the city does not need to be persuaded onto the glass just because the adults want a photograph." |
| ATTR-0004 | 592 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "View through the glass floor at the Seoul Sky observation deck" |
| MAIN-0134 | 593 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "The glass floor is a separate sensation from the skyline view — you do not need to step onto it to enjoy the observatory." |
| MAIN-0135 | 593 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[2] | "Photo:" |
| MAIN-0136 | 593 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Tristan Surtel / Wikimedia Commons" |
| MAIN-0137 | 593 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[3] | "·" |
| MAIN-0138 | 593 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/a[2]/text()[1] | "CC BY-SA 4.0" |
| MAIN-0139 | 593 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[4] | "· resized" |
| MAIN-0140 | 595 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Sky Terrace: an outdoor part of the visit" |
| MAIN-0141 | 596 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Sky Terrace is an outdoor terrace, not the separately booked Sky Bridge tour." |
| MAIN-0142 | 596 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "It adds an open-air stop to a visit otherwise centered on indoor viewing." |
| MAIN-0143 | 597 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Check whether the terrace is open when you arrive, especially if going outdoors matters to you. An observatory ticket should not be taken as a promise that every outdoor area will be available throughout your visit." |
| MAIN-0144 | 598 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "For a view-focused visit, we would treat the terrace as something to enjoy when available, rather than making the whole day depend on it. That leaves you free to spend more time with the indoor views when outdoor access is limited." |
| MAIN-0145 | 606 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Is the Sky Bridge Tour for You?" |
| MAIN-0146 | 609 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Sky Bridge is a separate outdoor tour high on the tower" |
| MAIN-0147 | 609 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | ", advertised at a height of 541 meters. Choose it for the experience of being outside at that height, rather than because you think ordinary admission is an incomplete visit." |
| MAIN-0148 | 610 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "It needs a deliberate place in the schedule. Treat it as a timed activity to prepare for, not a quick extra to squeeze in just before leaving." |
| MAIN-0149 | 611 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Before booking, read the current participation requirements for everyone who intends to join. Check the stated age and body-size limits, health and mobility conditions, required clothing and footwear, and instructions for personal belongings. An ordinary observatory visit is not evidence that someone qualifies for the bridge tour." |
| MAIN-0150 | 612 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "If only part of your group wants to participate, plan that split before paying. Agree where the others will spend the time and where you will meet afterward, without assuming that nonparticipants can accompany the tour." |
| MAIN-0151 | 613 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Weather can affect the outdoor experience. Read the operator's change or cancellation terms before placing another non-refundable booking immediately afterward. The fact that the indoor observatory is open does not establish that a particular bridge session will run." |
| MAIN-0152 | 614 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "For travelers whose main interest is the skyline, our choice remains the ordinary observatory visit. Sky Bridge earns its place when the outdoor experience itself is something you would be disappointed to miss." |
| MAIN-0153 | 619 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[13]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[1]/a[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check Sky Bridge tour availability and participation requirements" |
| MAIN-0154 | 628 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "123 Lounge: Make It Part of the Plan, Not a Required Stop" |
| MAIN-0155 | 631 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "123 Lounge offers food and drinks with the view from the uppermost floor." |
| MAIN-0156 | 631 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Consider it when sitting down for a meal or drinks is part of the occasion, rather than adding it simply to reach another floor." |
| MAIN-0157 | 632 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For a couple planning a celebration, the meal may deserve its own time in the day. For someone who mainly wants to look around and continue sightseeing, the observatory visit can be enough without a lounge stop." |
| MAIN-0158 | 633 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Before making a reservation or buying a package, check the relationship between the lounge booking and observatory admission. Confirm what the menu or package includes, the last order time, and whether any seating request is guaranteed. A preference for a window seat should remain a request unless the booking explicitly promises it." |
| MAIN-0159 | 634 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Do not budget on the assumption that buying one drink replaces an admission ticket. Compare the actual conditions and total cost of the visit you intend to make." |
| MAIN-0160 | 635 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[14]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "A view-focused stop and a meal with a view are different plans. Choose the one you want, then leave enough time to enjoy it." |
| MAIN-0161 | 643 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Visiting Seoul Sky With Children" |
| MAIN-0162 | 646 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "For a family visit, we would settle meals and any essential care before joining the admission line, then leave the length of the stay flexible. Choose a viewing time that fits your child's routine rather than treating sunset as compulsory." |
| MAIN-0163 | 647 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For parents of very young children, the practical question is how to get around without relying on a stroller. Resolve that before committing to a visit you cannot easily move." |
| MAIN-0164 | 648 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Bringing a stroller? Plan for time without it" |
| MAIN-0165 | 649 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Sky applies stroller restrictions and offers baby-carrier rental." |
| MAIN-0166 | 649 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Do not plan on keeping your child in a stroller throughout the visit." |
| MAIN-0167 | 650 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "The listed carrier rental is" |
| MAIN-0168 | 650 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "₩3,000 for children under 36 months" |
| MAIN-0169 | 650 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[2] | ". Confirm availability and whether the available carrier is suitable for your child; the age limit alone does not settle that question." |
| MAIN-0170 | 651 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Before joining the admission line, ask at the B1 information desk where your stroller needs to be left and how to collect it afterward. Put diapers, wipes and other essentials in a separate small bag so they do not stay behind in the stroller basket." |
| MAIN-0171 | 652 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "With a toddler who no longer uses a carrier but still tires easily, think through how much walking and carrying you are prepared to do. We would choose a time when the child is rested and keep the visit shorter, rather than relying on a stroller nap while waiting for the evening view." |
| MAIN-0172 | 653 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Feeding and changing: locate the facilities before going up" |
| MAIN-0173 | 654 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "An" |
| MAIN-0174 | 654 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Infant Lounge is listed on B1, in front of the VIP room" |
| MAIN-0175 | 654 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[2] | ". Ask staff about using it when you arrive, particularly if a feed or diaper change is due before admission." |
| MAIN-0176 | 655 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "Before heading upstairs, find out where to go for baby care during the viewing part of the visit. Knowing about a room in the entrance lobby does not tell you how to reach suitable facilities once you are on the upper floors." |
| MAIN-0177 | 656 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[15]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "Keep the next meal flexible enough that you can finish early if needed. There is little reason to prolong the visit just to reach a particular photograph or clock time." |
| MAIN-0178 | 664 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Accessibility: Ask for the Route Without Escalators" |
| MAIN-0179 | 667 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Sky provides facilities for wheelchair users and routes that avoid escalators." |
| MAIN-0180 | 667 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Tell staff what you need before following the main visitor flow, so they can explain the appropriate route between the viewing levels and back down." |
| MAIN-0181 | 668 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Free wheelchair rental is also listed. If a loan chair is essential to your visit, contact the venue beforehand about availability and arrangements rather than assuming one will be ready when you arrive." |
| MAIN-0182 | 669 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "For your own wheelchair or another mobility aid, describe the equipment when asking about access. Confirm the route you will use, where to find an accessible restroom and whether any assistance needs to be arranged. These are more useful questions than simply asking whether the building has elevators." |
| MAIN-0183 | 670 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "A loan chair at the venue also does not solve the journey from the station or your drop-off point. Plan that approach separately if walking distance is a concern." |
| MAIN-0184 | 671 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[16]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "This guidance concerns the observatory visit. Sky Bridge has separate participation requirements; access to the viewing floors does not establish eligibility for the outdoor tour." |
| MAIN-0185 | 679 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Visiting With Luggage or After Checkout" |
| MAIN-0186 | 682 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Arrange your bag storage before making Seoul Sky the next stop on checkout day." |
| MAIN-0187 | 682 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Our first choice is to ask your hotel about keeping the luggage, provided returning to collect it fits the rest of your route." |
| MAIN-0188 | 683 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If that would mean a long detour before a flight or train, look for storage along your onward journey instead. Confirm that it accepts your suitcase size and stays accessible until your planned collection time. Do not count on leaving a large case at the observatory without checking the arrangement first." |
| MAIN-0189 | 684 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "A stroller holding arrangement is not evidence of a large-suitcase storage service. Treat those as separate questions." |
| MAIN-0190 | 685 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Allow time both to drop the bags and to retrieve them. For an onward journey, work backward from when you need to leave for the airport or station; do not use that departure time as the moment to start leaving the viewing floors." |
| MAIN-0191 | 686 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[17]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "Keep your admission voucher, any ID required by the booking, valuables and essentials with you when storing the rest. The aim is a manageable visit, without a bag-collection deadline cutting short the view." |
| MAIN-0192 | 694 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Fitting Seoul Sky Into a Jamsil Day" |
| MAIN-0193 | 697 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "For a first visit centered on the view," |
| MAIN-0194 | 697 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "we would pair Seoul Sky with a meal and a short walk at Seokchon Lake" |
| MAIN-0195 | 697 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[2] | ". You can see the tower from ground level, spend time upstairs and still have room to eat without fitting in another major attraction." |
| ATTR-0005 | 700 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/img[1]/@alt | "Lotte World and Seokchon Lake seen from Seoul Sky" |
| MAIN-0196 | 701 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[1] | "Lotte World and Seokchon Lake are directly below the tower, but proximity does not mean they all belong in the same itinerary." |
| MAIN-0197 | 701 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[2] | "Photo:" |
| MAIN-0198 | 701 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Tristan Surtel / Wikimedia Commons" |
| MAIN-0199 | 701 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[3] | "·" |
| MAIN-0200 | 701 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/a[2]/text()[1] | "CC BY-SA 4.0" |
| MAIN-0201 | 701 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/figure[1]/figcaption[1]/text()[4] | "· resized" |
| MAIN-0202 | 703 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "A lake walk, a meal and the observatory" |
| MAIN-0203 | 704 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For a sunset visit, one suggested sequence is:" |
| MAIN-0204 | 705 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Part of Seokchon Lake → an early meal → Seoul Sky → return to your hotel." |
| MAIN-0205 | 706 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Keep the walk short enough that you can reach the entrance without rushing. You do not need to complete the whole lake before going up. A meal beforehand also avoids having to leave the viewing floors just as the evening view develops." |
| MAIN-0206 | 707 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "For daylight views, reverse the priorities rather than following that order mechanically. Go up while the conditions suit the visit, then decide how much of the lake or the surrounding area you want to see afterward." |
| MAIN-0207 | 708 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/h3[2]/text()[1] | "Adding the Aquarium" |
| MAIN-0208 | 709 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[6]/text()[1] | "The Aquarium belongs in this plan when someone in your group genuinely wants to visit it. With an evening view as the other priority, we would usually put the Aquarium earlier and leave a meal or proper break between the two, provided the ticket conditions allow that order." |
| MAIN-0209 | 710 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[7]/text()[1] | "A combination ticket is worth comparing once you have chosen both visits. Check the valid dates and any required visiting order before arranging the day around the package. Buying the combination should not be what persuades you to add a second attraction." |
| MAIN-0210 | 711 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[8]/text()[1] | "If visibility is poor, an Aquarium-and-mall day is an alternative for travelers interested in those activities. Already holding a combination ticket? Check its change and cancellation conditions before assuming you can move or recover the unused Seoul Sky portion." |
| MAIN-0211 | 712 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/p[9]/text()[1] | "For food, the lake and other ways to spend the time around your visit, continue with our neighborhood guide." |
| MAIN-0212 | 714 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[18]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Plan the rest of your Jamsil day" |
| MAIN-0213 | 723 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[19]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Can You Do Seoul Sky and Lotte World on the Same Day?" |
| MAIN-0214 | 726 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[19]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes, but decide which visit you are willing to shorten." |
| MAIN-0215 | 726 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[19]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "We would not make a fixed sunset visit the deadline for a full day of priority rides at Lotte World Adventure." |
| MAIN-0216 | 727 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[19]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "That plan can force a choice between a ride you have been waiting for and leaving in time for the observatory. A shorter park visit and Seoul Sky may fit together; a ride-focused day needs more freedom to absorb queues, meals and tired children." |
| MAIN-0217 | 728 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[19]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "If Seoul Sky is the main reason for coming, an Aquarium visit, a meal or a lake walk is easier to adjust around it than a full theme-park itinerary. If the rides matter more, protect that day and give the observatory a separate place in the trip when possible." |
| MAIN-0218 | 729 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[19]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Check exactly which attractions your tickets include. Ordinary Seoul Sky admission should not be treated as admission to Lotte World Adventure, or the other way around, unless the purchased package explicitly includes both." |
| MAIN-0219 | 731 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[19]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Plan your Lotte World visit" |
| MAIN-0220 | 740 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[20]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Before You Book Seoul Sky" |
| MAIN-0221 | 743 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[20]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Once you have chosen the kind of visit, compare the ticket that actually delivers it. For ordinary viewing, start with general admission. Consider Fast Pass for the priority-entry benefit, and Sky Bridge only when the outdoor tour itself is part of your plan." |
| MAIN-0222 | 744 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[20]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Use your intended date and the whole group's tickets when comparing the payable amount. Confirm when the voucher will be ready, whether it needs exchanging, and whether its validity and cancellation conditions fit the plan. There is no saving in a cheaper ticket you cannot use as intended." |
| MAIN-0223 | 745 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[20]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Ready to go? Check the current options for your date and book the visit you have chosen." |
| MAIN-0224 | 756 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[20]/div[1]/div[1]/aside[1]/p[1]/a[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check Seoul Sky tickets for your date" |
| MAIN-0225 | 765 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "STAY GUIDE" |
| MAIN-0226 | 766 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Choose a Seoul Base for the Whole Trip" |
| MAIN-0227 | 767 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "A visit to Seoul Sky is not a reason on its own to move hotels to Jamsil. You can plan it as a day out from a base in Hongdae, Myeongdong, Gangnam or another area that suits the rest of your trip." |
| MAIN-0228 | 768 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "A Jamsil stay deserves more attention when several days revolve around its attractions or nearby events. For a single observatory visit, compare the whole trip: airport travel, daily journeys, room requirements and where you want to return at night." |
| MAIN-0229 | 769 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/aside[1]/div[1]/div[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Compare where to stay in Seoul" |
| MAIN-0230 | 777 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Sky FAQ" |
| MAIN-0231 | 781 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Is Seoul Sky the same as Lotte World Tower?" |
| MAIN-0232 | 782 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Sky is the paid observatory inside Lotte World Tower. The tower and mall are the wider complex; Lotte World Adventure is the separate amusement park. Check the attraction name on the ticket you buy." |
| MAIN-0233 | 785 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "How long should I allow for Seoul Sky?" |
| MAIN-0234 | 786 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "We suggest around 90–120 minutes for an ordinary observatory visit, with travel and substantial queues on top. This is a planning allowance, not an official duration. Allow longer for the change from daylight to night, a meal or a Sky Bridge booking." |
| MAIN-0235 | 789 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Is sunset always the best time to go?" |
| MAIN-0236 | 790 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/p[1]/text()[1] | "No. Choose sunset for the change from daylight to the evening skyline. Clear daylight can be a better choice when seeing the city in detail matters more. Let the conditions and your schedule guide the decision." |
| MAIN-0237 | 793 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Do I need Fast Pass?" |
| MAIN-0238 | 794 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Not automatically. General admission is our starting choice for ordinary viewing. Consider Fast Pass when priority entry would solve a real waiting problem for your group, and read the selected ticket's benefits before paying more." |
| MAIN-0239 | 797 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Does a QR ticket let me skip every queue?" |
| MAIN-0240 | 798 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/p[1]/text()[1] | "No. QR is a ticket or voucher format, not proof of priority admission. Direct-entry instructions may let you bypass ticket exchange; that is different from Fast Pass. Follow the instructions attached to your booking." |
| MAIN-0241 | 801 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "What if every date looks sold out online?" |
| MAIN-0242 | 802 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Check the selected product and booking period, then ask Seoul Sky or the seller about the date you need. A calendar showing no availability is not enough to establish whether other ticket options are available. Do not assume either guaranteed entry or a website error." |
| MAIN-0243 | 805 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[7]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Can I combine Seoul Sky and the Aquarium?" |
| MAIN-0244 | 806 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[7]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes, when you want both visits and have left time for each. Confirm the package's valid dates and visiting order before booking. We would not add a full Lotte World Adventure day to that plan by default." |
| MAIN-0245 | 809 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[8]/summary[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Do I need to stay in Jamsil to visit Seoul Sky?" |
| MAIN-0246 | 810 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[21]/div[1]/div[1]/details[8]/p[1]/text()[1] | "No. Choose your hotel around the whole Seoul trip. Staying in Jamsil is a separate decision based on how often you expect to use the area, not a requirement for one observatory visit." |

| HEAD-0001 | 6 | /html[1]/head[1]/meta[3]/@description | "Compare hotels near Seoul Station for KTX, AREX, early trains, business trips and family stays, with practical trade-offs before you book." |
| HEAD-0002 | 15 | /html[1]/head[1]/title[1]/text()[1] | "Hotels Near Seoul Station for KTX & AREX \| Korea Inside" |
| SCHEMA-0001 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/0/name | "Is Seoul Station a good area to stay in Seoul?" |
| SCHEMA-0002 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/0/acceptedAnswer/text | "Yes, when KTX, AREX, an early train, or travel to other Korean cities is an important part of your itinerary. If most of your trip is spent sightseeing in Seoul, Myeongdong, Hongdae, or Jongno may be a better base." |
| SCHEMA-0003 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/1/name | "Should I stay near Seoul Station before an early KTX?" |
| SCHEMA-0004 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/1/acceptedAnswer/text | "Usually yes. Staying nearby removes the need to cross Seoul early in the morning. Still leave extra time inside the station—the entrance and the KTX platform are not the same thing." |
| SCHEMA-0005 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/2/name | "Is Seoul Station convenient for Incheon Airport?" |
| SCHEMA-0006 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/2/acceptedAnswer/text | "Yes. AREX connects Seoul Station with Incheon Airport, making the area particularly practical for a first or final night in Seoul or for trips that combine a flight with KTX travel." |
| SCHEMA-0007 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/3/name | "Is Myeongdong better than Seoul Station for first-time visitors?" |
| SCHEMA-0008 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/3/acceptedAnswer/text | "For a first trip focused mainly on Seoul sightseeing and shopping, often yes. Seoul Station becomes the stronger choice when intercity rail or airport connections are a significant part of the trip." |
| SCHEMA-0009 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/4/name | "Can families stay comfortably near Seoul Station?" |
| SCHEMA-0010 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/4/acceptedAnswer/text | "Yes, but room layout matters. Some station-area hotels have very compact rooms, while UH Suite, Ramada, and Travel House offer options better suited to larger groups. Check the actual beds and room configuration rather than the guest limit alone." |
| SCHEMA-0011 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/5/name | "Do I need to stay right beside Seoul Station to use KTX?" |
| SCHEMA-0012 | 364 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/mainEntity/5/acceptedAnswer/text | "No. Hotels around Namdaemun and City Hall can still work if you use KTX only occasionally. For an early departure or one-night rail connection, however, staying closer to the station has more value." |

| MAIN-0006 | 486 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Station is not the default place I would choose for a first trip spent entirely in Seoul. Its advantage is what happens before and after your stay: KTX trains to other Korean cities, AREX to Incheon Airport, and an easier start when you have an early train or a work trip outside Seoul." |
| MAIN-0007 | 487 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If your itinerary includes Busan, Gyeongju, Daejeon, Daegu, or another city by rail, staying nearby can remove an unnecessary cross-Seoul trip on travel day. The same applies if you arrive on the AREX and plan to continue by KTX, or return to Seoul for one night before flying home." |
| MAIN-0008 | 488 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "For travelers spending most of their time sightseeing, shopping, eating out, and staying out late in Seoul, Myeongdong, Hongdae, or Jongno may still be a better base." |

| MAIN-0013 | 506 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "Your itinerary connects Incheon Airport, Seoul, and another Korean city." |
| MAIN-0014 | 507 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/ul[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "You are in Seoul for work and expect to travel to other cities by rail." |
| MAIN-0015 | 508 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/ul[1]/li[5]/text()[1] | "This is your first or last night in Seoul and transport matters more than neighborhood atmosphere." |
| MAIN-0016 | 513 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Look elsewhere if:" |
| MAIN-0017 | 515 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/ul[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "You are staying in Seoul for several days and have little or no reason to use KTX." |
| MAIN-0018 | 516 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/ul[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "You want cafés, shopping, restaurants, and nightlife immediately outside your hotel." |
| MAIN-0019 | 517 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "Most of your sightseeing is around Myeongdong, the palaces, Jongno, or Hongdae and you would be commuting back to Seoul Station every night." |
| MAIN-0020 | 522 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "The practical rule:" |
| MAIN-0021 | 522 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[2] | "Stay near Seoul Station because it simplifies the next part of your trip—not simply because it is a major station." |
| ATTR-0002 | 526 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/@aria-label | "Who Seoul Station does and does not suit" |
| MAIN-0022 | 530 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Who Seoul Station is actually for" |
| MAIN-0023 | 532 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Station earns its place in an itinerary when the station is doing real work for the trip. An early KTX is the obvious case, but the same logic applies when Seoul is one stop in a longer journey: arriving from Incheon Airport, staying overnight, then continuing to another city the next day." |
| MAIN-0024 | 533 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Business travel creates a similar pattern. If meetings take you between Seoul and other cities, staying near the station can remove an extra subway or taxi ride each time you travel. In that situation, a quieter neighborhood outside the hotel may matter less than being able to start the next rail leg without crossing Seoul first." |
| MAIN-0025 | 534 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Families and groups have another reason to consider the area. Moving several people on a fixed train schedule is different from a solo traveler catching one KTX. A larger room, suite, or apartment near the station can simplify the whole departure morning even if the hotel is not the closest building to the tracks." |
| MAIN-0026 | 539 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Why not everyone should stay here" |
| MAIN-0027 | 541 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "A hotel near Seoul Station is less convincing when the station appears only once in the itinerary. If most days are spent around the palaces, Jongno, Myeongdong, Seongsu, or Hongdae, choosing a hotel here can simply replace one convenient train day with several days of unnecessary commuting." |
| MAIN-0028 | 542 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The neighborhood itself is another consideration. Seoul Station is busy and practical, but it does not give you the same evening experience as walking out into Hongdae’s restaurants and nightlife or staying within Myeongdong’s shopping streets. Travelers who want the area around the hotel to feel like part of the trip may be happier elsewhere." |
| MAIN-0029 | 543 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "And proximity should not be judged by luggage alone. A backpacker may barely care about a short staircase or an extra five-minute walk, while a family with several suitcases may care a great deal. The useful question is not “Which hotel is closest to Seoul Station?” but “Does staying here improve enough of my itinerary to justify choosing this area?”" |
| MAIN-0030 | 553 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "What “near Seoul Station” really means" |
| MAIN-0031 | 554 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/header[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "A hotel can be only a few hundred meters from Seoul Station and still feel quite different depending on which side of the station it sits on. Seoul Station is a large transport complex, so the useful question is not simply “How close is the hotel?” but “Which side of the station will I actually use?”" |
| MAIN-0032 | 560 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Immediate station area" |
| MAIN-0033 | 563 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Stay closest to the station when the train itself is the main reason for being here: an early KTX, a one-night connection, or a short business stop." |
| MAIN-0034 | 564 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The advantage is obvious, but do not read “near Seoul Station” as “beside the platform.” You still have to move through the station, find the correct rail area, and reach the right exit. For a short overnight that is usually acceptable; for a longer Seoul stay, proximity alone is not much of a reason to choose the neighborhood." |
| MAIN-0035 | 570 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "West and Exit 15 side" |
| MAIN-0036 | 573 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "The west side around Exit 15 leads toward Malli-dong and several apartment-style and smaller stays." |
| MAIN-0037 | 574 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "This side is worth looking at when you want more than a standard hotel room—especially a suite, apartment, kitchen, or washing machine. It can work well for families and groups moving between cities. The streets behind the station are more residential than the main eastern frontage, and some routes include slopes or smaller side streets, so check the exact property rather than treating every Exit 15 stay as interchangeable." |
| MAIN-0038 | 580 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Seoullo and Namdaemun side" |
| MAIN-0039 | 583 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "This is the better compromise when Seoul Station matters, but you still want the hotel to connect naturally with central sightseeing." |
| MAIN-0040 | 584 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Hotels here are generally less about getting the shortest possible walk to a train and more about keeping both parts of the trip workable: KTX when you need it, then Namdaemun, Myeongdong or central Seoul on the other days." |
| MAIN-0041 | 590 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "City Hall boundary" |
| MAIN-0042 | 593 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "By the time a hotel is closer to City Hall or deeper into Namdaemun, I would stop thinking of it as a true “station hotel.”" |
| MAIN-0043 | 594 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "That does not make it a bad choice. For a business traveler with meetings around City Hall, or someone spending several days sightseeing and using KTX only once or twice, this location can actually work better." |
| MAIN-0044 | 595 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "But if the whole reason for booking near Seoul Station is an early train or a tight overnight connection, this is the point where I would compare the full morning route against the closer options before booking." |
| MAIN-0045 | 605 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Eight hotels near Seoul Station, matched to the trip" |
| MAIN-0046 | 611 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "FULL-SERVICE STATION BASE" |
| MAIN-0047 | 612 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Four Points by Sheraton Josun, Seoul Station" |
| MAIN-0048 | 613 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "KTX · AREX · business · first or last night" |
| MAIN-0049 | 616 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Four Points is the hotel I would start with when Seoul Station itself is the reason for the stay. It works particularly well for a business trip, an overnight between Incheon Airport and a KTX journey, or the final night in Seoul before heading to the airport. Unlike the smaller stays around the station, you are getting a conventional full-service hotel with a 24-hour hotel environment, restaurants, a fitness center, and rooms with proper work desks and Wi-Fi." |
| MAIN-0050 | 617 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The important detail is what “connected to Seoul Station” actually means. The hotel connects to the station through the underground passage at Exit 12, but the walk from Seoul Station is still roughly 8–15 minutes. KTX, AREX, GTX-A and subway passengers still have to move through a large station complex before reaching the hotel side. That indoor route is useful in bad weather, but this is not a hotel where you step off the train and walk straight into the lobby." |
| MAIN-0051 | 618 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "I would be less enthusiastic about paying for Four Points if you only need a bed for a few hours before an early train, or if most of the trip is about cafés, nightlife and sightseeing elsewhere in Seoul. Rooms can also feel compact once several suitcases are open. In those cases, the value of the full-service hotel has to matter enough to justify choosing it over a smaller station stay or a more interesting neighborhood." |

| MAIN-0056 | 625 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[3]/text()[1] | "Agoda" |
| MAIN-0057 | 633 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "COMPACT SOLO OVERNIGHT" |
| MAIN-0058 | 634 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Hotelette Seoul Station" |
| MAIN-0059 | 635 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Solo · early KTX · short stay · backpack or carry-on" |
| MAIN-0060 | 638 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Hotelette is the kind of place to consider when the night is mostly about sleeping close to Seoul Station and leaving again the next morning. It sits right by Exit 10, and for a solo traveler with a backpack or small carry-on, that proximity can matter more than having a large room or full hotel facilities." |
| MAIN-0061 | 639 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The room is the limiting factor, not the location. The rooms are very compact, and a full-size suitcase can quickly take over the available floor space. The hotel itself has an elevator, but Exit 10 includes a short flight of stairs. That is manageable with light luggage and much less appealing with a large case." |
| MAIN-0062 | 640 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "There is also a clear family filter: children are not allowed, and there are no cots or extra beds. This is therefore not a small family hotel disguised as a budget option. It is a narrow-purpose stay for one adult who values the station more than room space." |
| MAIN-0063 | 642 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "CHECK RATES" |

| MAIN-0068 | 655 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "COMPACT STAY FOR 2–4" |
| MAIN-0069 | 656 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "ZIYOLK Seoul Station" |
| MAIN-0070 | 657 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Couple · small group · short rail stay · early KTX" |
| MAIN-0071 | 660 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "ZIYOLK fills a gap that Hotelette does not. The rooms are still compact, but there is a real four-person option: the Family Room is 21㎡ with two double beds, while the Double and Twin rooms are 10㎡ and 12㎡. That makes it worth considering for a couple or small group that wants to stay very close to Seoul Station without moving up to a larger full-service hotel." |
| MAIN-0072 | 661 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Exit 10 is the shortest approach for most guests. The catch is the final approach: there is a short but fairly steep slope, and Exit 10 itself is not the easiest choice with heavy luggage." |
| MAIN-0073 | 662 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "I would choose ZIYOLK for a short rail-focused stay rather than for room space. The rooms fill up quickly once a suitcase is opened. Self-service check-in outside staffed hours can help with a late arrival, but soundproofing and the compact layout are reasons not to treat the Family Room as a roomy family hotel." |

| MAIN-0079 | 677 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "STATION + CITY BALANCE" |
| MAIN-0080 | 678 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Hotel Manu Seoul" |
| MAIN-0081 | 679 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Couple · sightseeing + KTX · short business stay" |
| MAIN-0082 | 682 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "If Seoul Station matters but you do not want the station to dominate the whole stay, Hotel Manu is where the balance starts to shift. It sits on the Sungnyemun and Namdaemun side rather than directly beside the platforms, so you can still use KTX while having a more natural route toward Namdaemun Market, Myeongdong and central Seoul." |
| MAIN-0083 | 683 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The walk needs a little explanation. One practical route is to use the outdoor elevator up to Seoullo 7017 and follow the elevated walkway toward the hotel, which can be easier with luggage than blindly following the nearest station exit. But “close to Seoul Station” still does not mean close to the KTX platform. The size of the station complex adds time after you reach the station itself." |
| MAIN-0084 | 684 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "That is why I would not pick Manu for a dawn departure when shaving every possible minute off the station approach is the priority. Its stronger case is a mixed itinerary—one or two rail trips, then meals, sightseeing or business in central Seoul. Room type still matters because some rooms are compact." |

| MAIN-0091 | 700 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Hotel Gracery Seoul" |
| MAIN-0092 | 701 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Business · City Hall · central sightseeing · occasional KTX" |
| MAIN-0093 | 704 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Hotel Gracery only belongs in this guide when Seoul Station is part of the trip rather than the whole reason for the stay. The hotel is much more naturally tied to City Hall and Namdaemun. Seoul Station is walkable, but this is enough distance that I would not treat it as a station-front hotel." |
| MAIN-0094 | 705 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Where Gracery becomes more convincing is a business or mixed city itinerary. The hotel has a business center with laptops and printing, coin laundry, a 24-hour front desk, and self-service luggage lockers." |
| MAIN-0095 | 706 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "I would choose it when meetings around City Hall or central Seoul occupy most of the stay and KTX is needed only once or twice. I would not choose it simply because a booking site labels it “near Seoul Station,” especially before an early train. Gracery earns its place by giving the days in Seoul more convenience, not by giving you the shortest route to a KTX platform." |

| MAIN-0116 | 749 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The airport connection is the other reason to look at it. | MAIN-0125 | 767 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[8]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Family · group · 4–6 people · laundry · multi-city stay" |
| MAIN-0126 | 770 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[8]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Travel House is the option I would look at when a hotel room stops being the most practical setup. For a family or group spending several nights in Seoul between trips to other Korean cities, being able to wash clothes and eat something simple in the apartment can matter more than having a hotel lobby or daily full-service facilities." |
| MAIN-0127 | 771 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[8]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The property is on the west side of Seoul Station, around the Exit 15 approach. I would not publish a precise platform-to-door time because map estimates vary. The useful point is that this is part of the station’s west-side apartment cluster." |
| MAIN-0128 | 772 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[8]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Group capacity needs a reality check, though. Check the exact bed layout, bathroom count and apartment type before booking—especially for a larger family." |
