| MAIN-0133 | 779 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/article[8]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[3]/text()[1] | "Agoda" |
| MAIN-0134 | 791 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "KTX, AREX and the Reality of Using Seoul Station" |
| MAIN-0135 | 797 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "For KTX, plan around the station — not just the hotel address" |
| MAIN-0136 | 800 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "If you are staying here for an early KTX, do not time the morning from the hotel door to the nearest station entrance. Seoul Station is a large complex, and reaching the building is only the first part of the trip. You still need to find the main rail concourse, check the departure board and reach the correct platform." |
| MAIN-0137 | 801 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "That matters most with hotels on the edges of the area. A property may be described as ten minutes from Seoul Station, but that does not mean ten minutes to your KTX seat. For an important departure, I would leave some margin rather than trying to turn a map estimate into the latest possible checkout time." |
| MAIN-0138 | 807 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "AREX has two different train experiences" |
| MAIN-0139 | 810 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Do not treat every train marked AREX as the same airport service." |
| MAIN-0140 | 811 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The Express Train runs between Seoul Station and Incheon Airport Terminals 1 and 2 with reserved seating and its own fare system. The All-Stop Train uses unreserved seating and stops at intermediate stations." |
| MAIN-0141 | 812 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "The physical layout is another reason not to rush a connection. The airport railroad facilities are on multiple underground levels, with the platform much deeper than the main station concourse. Arriving at Seoul Station by KTX does not put you immediately beside the AREX train." |
| MAIN-0142 | 818 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "The City Airport Terminal can make the final night more useful" |
| MAIN-0143 | 821 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Station has another advantage that is easy to overlook: the City Airport Terminal." |
| MAIN-0144 | 822 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For eligible passengers using the AREX Express Train, the terminal allows airport check-in, checked-baggage drop and departure immigration to be completed at Seoul Station before traveling to Incheon Airport." |
| MAIN-0145 | 823 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "That can make a final night near Seoul Station more attractive than the hotel distance alone suggests. Do check the current airline eligibility and operating conditions before building the day around this service." |
| MAIN-0146 | 829 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "A transfer here is convenient, but it is still a transfer" |
| MAIN-0147 | 832 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Station makes KTX and airport travel easier to connect. It does not make the physical connection disappear." |
| MAIN-0148 | 833 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For someone with one backpack and plenty of time, the internal walk may barely register. For a family, an older traveler, or anyone trying to connect a train with a flight on a tight schedule, the same station can feel very different." |
| MAIN-0149 | 834 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "That is why the hotel recommendations in this guide do not rank properties by meters from Seoul Station alone." |
| MAIN-0150 | 844 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "How Seoul Station Fits Into a Real Korea Trip" |
| MAIN-0151 | 850 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Arriving from Incheon, then taking KTX the next morning" |
| MAIN-0152 | 851 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If your flight lands in the afternoon or evening and the next part of the trip is Busan, Gyeongju, Daegu, or another rail destination, one night near Seoul Station can keep the first day simple. Take the AREX into Seoul, check in, eat nearby, and start the intercity trip the next morning without moving across the city again." |
| MAIN-0153 | 852 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "For a late arrival, I would pay more attention to the hotel’s check-in arrangements than to whether it is two or five minutes closer to the station." |
| MAIN-0154 | 855 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Returning from another city before an international flight" |
| MAIN-0155 | 856 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If you are coming back from Busan or another city and flying out the following day, staying near Seoul Station gives you some separation between the intercity journey and the flight." |
| MAIN-0156 | 857 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "You are no longer depending on a same-day KTX arrival, a cross-Seoul transfer, and an international departure all going exactly to plan." |
| MAIN-0157 | 858 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/p[4]/text()[1] | "That extra night is particularly worth considering around Korean public holidays, when intercity train seats can become difficult to secure. Check the current KORAIL booking schedule rather than relying on an old holiday timetable." |
| MAIN-0158 | 861 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/p[1]/text()[1] | "A work trip that moves between Seoul and other cities" |
| MAIN-0159 | 862 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/p[2]/text()[1] | "A business traveler may use Seoul Station very differently from a tourist." |
| MAIN-0160 | 863 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/p[3]/text()[1] | "If one day is in central Seoul and the next involves a KTX trip for meetings elsewhere, staying around the station can remove an unnecessary commute before the workday even begins." |
| MAIN-0161 | 864 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/p[4]/text()[1] | "The decision still depends on where the work actually is. If every meeting is in Gangnam, staying near Seoul Station just because KTX is available there may create more commuting than it saves." |
| MAIN-0162 | 867 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/p[1]/text()[1] | "A family or group moving through several cities" |
| MAIN-0163 | 868 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For four or five people, the morning before a train is less about shaving two minutes off the walk and more about getting everyone out of the room, finding the right platform, and keeping the group together." |
| MAIN-0164 | 869 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/p[3]/text()[1] | "That is where properties such as UH Suite, Ramada, or Travel House enter the picture. Separate bedrooms, several real beds, an airport bus, or apartment facilities may solve more problems for a family than simply booking the hotel with the shortest map distance to Seoul Station." |
| MAIN-0165 | 872 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Staying here while still sightseeing in Seoul" |
| MAIN-0166 | 873 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Using KTX does not mean every night has to be built around the station." |
| MAIN-0167 | 874 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/p[3]/text()[1] | "If you have one intercity trip during a five-day Seoul stay, Hotel Manu or the Namdaemun/City Hall side can be a better compromise than sleeping beside the station entrance." |
| MAIN-0168 | 875 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/p[4]/text()[1] | "If KTX appears only once and the rest of the itinerary is concentrated around Hongdae, Myeongdong or Jongno, compare those neighborhoods before deciding that Seoul Station needs to be your base at all." |
| MAIN-0169 | 885 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "What to check before you book" |
| MAIN-0170 | 887 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check the room, not just the guest limit." |
| MAIN-0171 | 887 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "A room that allows four people may still mean two double beds in a compact space. For families and groups, check the actual bed configuration before comparing prices." |
| MAIN-0172 | 888 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check which side of Seoul Station the hotel is on." |
| MAIN-0173 | 888 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "Two hotels can both be described as “near Seoul Station” but lead to very different walks. The station exit and the direction you will normally use matter more than the straight-line distance." |
| MAIN-0174 | 889 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check late-arrival rules if your flight lands at night." |
| MAIN-0175 | 889 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "Full-service hotels are straightforward, but some smaller properties use limited reception hours or self check-in. Do not assume every Seoul Station stay has a 24-hour front desk." |
| MAIN-0176 | 890 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check the airport connection you actually plan to use." |
| MAIN-0177 | 890 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "AREX is not automatically the best choice for every hotel. Ramada, for example, can make more sense for some groups because of the airport limousine stop." |
| MAIN-0178 | 891 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "For apartment-style stays, check the exact unit." |
| MAIN-0179 | 891 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/text()[1] | "At properties such as Travel House or UH Suite, room layouts can differ substantially. Bedroom count, bathroom count and living space are more important than the property name alone." |
| MAIN-0180 | 900 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Final Recommendation" |
| MAIN-0181 | 903 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "If Seoul Station is central to your trip, start with Four Points. It is the most straightforward choice for KTX, AREX, business travel, and a first or final night in Seoul." |
| MAIN-0182 | 904 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For a short solo stay before an early train, Hotelette is the simpler option. If two to four people want to stay very close to the station, ZIYOLK gives you more flexibility without moving far away." |
| MAIN-0183 | 905 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "If you still want central Seoul to feel like part of the trip, look at Hotel Manu. For business around City Hall with only occasional KTX use, Hotel Gracery is the better fit." |
| MAIN-0184 | 906 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Families and groups should choose by room setup rather than distance alone. UH Suite works better when separate bedrooms matter, Ramada when hotel service and the airport limousine matter, and Travel House when an apartment-style stay is more useful." |
| MAIN-0185 | 907 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "If none of those reasons describe your itinerary, Seoul Station probably does not need to be your base at all. Myeongdong, Hongdae, or Jongno may give you a better stay in Seoul." |
| MAIN-0186 | 915 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Hotels Near Seoul Station FAQ" |
| MAIN-0187 | 919 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Is Seoul Station a good area to stay in Seoul?" |
| MAIN-0188 | 920 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes, when KTX, AREX, an early train, or travel to other Korean cities is an important part of your itinerary. If most of your trip is spent sightseeing in Seoul, Myeongdong, Hongdae, or Jongno may be a better base." |
| MAIN-0189 | 923 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Should I stay near Seoul Station before an early KTX?" |
| MAIN-0190 | 924 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Usually yes. Staying nearby removes the need to cross Seoul early in the morning. Still leave extra time inside the station—the entrance and the KTX platform are not the same thing." |
| MAIN-0191 | 927 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Is Seoul Station convenient for Incheon Airport?" |
| MAIN-0192 | 928 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes. AREX connects Seoul Station with Incheon Airport, making the area particularly practical for a first or final night in Seoul or for trips that combine a flight with KTX travel." |
| MAIN-0193 | 931 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Is Myeongdong better than Seoul Station for first-time visitors?" |
| MAIN-0194 | 932 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/p[1]/text()[1] | "For a first trip focused mainly on Seoul sightseeing and shopping, often yes. Seoul Station becomes the stronger choice when intercity rail or airport connections are a significant part of the trip." |
| MAIN-0195 | 935 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Can families stay comfortably near Seoul Station?" |
| MAIN-0196 | 936 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes, but room layout matters. Some station-area hotels have very compact rooms, while UH Suite, Ramada, and Travel House offer options better suited to larger groups. Check the actual beds and room configuration rather than the guest limit alone." |
| MAIN-0197 | 939 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Do I need to stay right beside Seoul Station to use KTX?" |
| MAIN-0198 | 940 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/div[1]/details[6]/p[1]/text()[1] | "No. Hotels around Namdaemun and City Hall can still work if you use KTX only occasionally. For an early departure or one-night rail connection, however, staying closer to the station has more value." |

| SCHEMA-0003 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/0/name | "Is Gongdeok a good area to stay in Seoul?" |
| SCHEMA-0004 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/0/acceptedAnswer/text | "Yes, especially when airport access, business travel, or moving between several parts of Seoul matters. For a first trip focused mainly on palaces, shopping and central sightseeing, Myeongdong or Jongno may be more convenient." |
| SCHEMA-0005 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/1/name | "Does the AREX Express stop at Gongdeok?" |
| SCHEMA-0006 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/1/acceptedAnswer/text | "No. Gongdeok is served by the AREX All-Stop Train. The Express service runs between Incheon Airport and Seoul Station." |
| SCHEMA-0007 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/2/name | "Is Gongdeok better than Hongdae for accommodation?" |
| SCHEMA-0008 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/2/acceptedAnswer/text | "It depends on the trip. Hongdae is better when nightlife, cafés and neighborhood atmosphere are priorities. Gongdeok is quieter and stronger as a transport and business base." |
| SCHEMA-0009 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/3/name | "Should I stay near Gongdeok Station or Mapo Station?" |
| SCHEMA-0010 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/3/acceptedAnswer/text | "Choose Gongdeok when AREX and multiple rail lines are important. Mapo Station can work better when you prefer a particular hotel, family room, or the Han River side and do not need the Gongdeok interchange every day." |
| SCHEMA-0011 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/4/name | "Is Gongdeok good for families?" |
| SCHEMA-0012 | 175 | /html[1]/head[1]/script[1]/@/@graph/1/mainEntity/4/acceptedAnswer/text | "It can be. LOTTE City Hotel Mapo, Seoul Garden Hotel, Hotel Naru and Gongdeok Stay Masil cover different family and group sizes. Check the exact beds, occupancy and bathroom setup rather than assuming every room works equally well for a family." |

| MAIN-0003 | 304 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Seoul Stay Guide" |
| MAIN-0004 | 305 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/h1[1]/text()[1] | "Hotels Near Gongdeok Station" |
| MAIN-0005 | 305 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/h1[1]/span[1]/text()[1] | "2026" |
| MAIN-0006 | 307 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok is worth considering when your Seoul itinerary pulls you in several directions. AREX All-Stop trains connect it with Incheon Airport, while Lines 5 and 6 and the Gyeongui-Jungang Line make it easier to move toward Yeouido, central Seoul, Hongdae, and other parts of the city without changing hotels." |
| MAIN-0007 | 308 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "It is also a good alternative for travelers who want Hongdae within easy reach but do not want its nightlife outside the hotel every night. Around Gongdeok and nearby Mapo Station, the accommodation mix ranges from straightforward business hotels to family rooms, a Han River luxury stay, and a private three-bedroom option." |
| MAIN-0008 | 309 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[1]/div[1]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "I would not choose Gongdeok just because it has several train lines. If most of your trip is a first-time sightseeing itinerary around Myeongdong, Jongno, and the palaces—or if nightlife is the main reason for choosing a neighborhood—another part of Seoul will usually be more satisfying." |

| MAIN-0022 | 334 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Should You Stay Near Gongdeok Station?" |
| MAIN-0023 | 338 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Choose Gongdeok if:" |

| MAIN-0025 | 341 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/ul[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "Your schedule includes Yeouido, central Seoul, or business appointments in more than one part of the city." |
| MAIN-0026 | 342 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "You want to visit Hongdae without sleeping in its busiest nightlife streets." |
| MAIN-0027 | 343 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/ul[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "A calmer evening base matters more than having major sights outside the hotel." |
| MAIN-0028 | 344 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/ul[1]/li[5]/text()[1] | "You need to compare business rooms, suites, family rooms, and group stays in one area." |
| MAIN-0029 | 348 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Look elsewhere if:" |
| MAIN-0030 | 350 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/ul[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "Your first Seoul trip is built mainly around palaces, Jongno, Myeongdong, and central sightseeing." |
| MAIN-0031 | 351 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/ul[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "Nightlife and late cafés are the main reason for choosing a neighborhood." |
| MAIN-0032 | 352 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/ul[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "You want to step outside the hotel and immediately feel part of a major visitor district." |
| MAIN-0033 | 353 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/ul[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "Most of your days are in Gangnam, Jamsil, or southeastern Seoul." |
| MAIN-0034 | 357 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[2]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Stay in Gongdeok when its transport connections make several days of your trip easier, not just the ride from the airport." |
| MAIN-0035 | 364 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Why Travelers Stay Around Gongdeok" |
| MAIN-0036 | 365 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/header[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Airport access gets most of the attention, but Gongdeok becomes more convincing when that convenience also helps with work, longer stays, or days that cross different parts of Seoul." |
| MAIN-0037 | 369 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Airport access without changing neighborhoods" |
| MAIN-0038 | 371 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok is on the AREX All-Stop route between Incheon Airport and Seoul. That can make arrival day simple, especially when the same hotel location still works for the rest of your itinerary." |
| MAIN-0039 | 372 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The distinction matters: the AREX Express does not stop at Gongdeok. If the nonstop reserved-seat service is important to you, Seoul Station is the better starting point." |
| MAIN-0040 | 376 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Several lines, but for different jobs" |
| MAIN-0041 | 378 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Lines 5 and 6, the Gyeongui-Jungang Line, and AREX all meet at Gongdeok. The value is not the number of lines by itself. It is being able to reach places such as Yeouido, central Seoul, and Hongdae from the same base without rebuilding your route every day." |
| MAIN-0042 | 382 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "A business district that still has an evening" |
| MAIN-0043 | 384 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Mapo-daero feels like a working district, with offices and business hotels along the main road. A few streets away, Gongdeok Market and the smaller restaurant streets give the area somewhere to go after work without turning it into another nightlife district." |
| MAIN-0044 | 385 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[3]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "That balance tends to suit repeat visitors and business travelers better than someone expecting major sightseeing outside the hotel door." |
| MAIN-0045 | 395 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok Station vs Mapo Station" |
| MAIN-0046 | 396 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/header[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok and Mapo are only one stop apart on Line 5, but they work differently as hotel bases. Gongdeok is the stronger transport hub; Mapo becomes more interesting when the hotel itself, family room choices, or the Han River side matter more." |
| MAIN-0047 | 400 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "MULTI-LINE INTERCHANGE" |
| MAIN-0048 | 401 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok Station" |
| MAIN-0049 | 402 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Choose Gongdeok when AREX or several subway lines are part of the reason for staying here. GLAD Mapo, LOTTE City Hotel Mapo, Roynet Hotel Seoul Mapo, Shilla Stay Mapo, and Gongdeok Stay Masil all sit more naturally in this transport-focused group." |
| MAIN-0050 | 403 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "The station is large, so the closest hotel on a map is not automatically the easiest for every line. Check the exit you will actually use, especially if airport access or luggage matters." |
| MAIN-0051 | 406 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "LINE 5 + HAN RIVER SIDE" |
| MAIN-0052 | 407 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Mapo Station" |
| MAIN-0053 | 408 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Mapo Station is a simpler Line 5 base. Seoul Garden Hotel and Hotel Naru fit this side better, where the decision is less about having four rail lines underneath you and more about the room, the hotel, or proximity to the Han River." |
| MAIN-0054 | 409 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[4]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "If you expect to use AREX repeatedly, staying near Mapo Station means going back to Gongdeok first. That is a small inconvenience for some trips and an unnecessary one for others." |
| MAIN-0055 | 418 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Who Should Not Stay Here" |
| MAIN-0056 | 421 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok is less convincing when transport is the only thing it improves. If your first Seoul trip is mostly palaces, Jongno, Myeongdong, shopping, and central sightseeing, staying closer to those areas may save more time over the whole trip." |
| MAIN-0057 | 422 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The same applies if nightlife is a major part of the plan. Hongdae is close, but returning to Gongdeok after every late evening is still a commute. If cafés, music, and busy streets are part of what you want outside the hotel, stay in Hongdae instead." |
| MAIN-0058 | 423 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[5]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "And do not choose Gongdeok for the airport alone. One convenient AREX ride at the beginning and end of a week-long trip may not outweigh several days of traveling back and forth to the places you actually came to see." |
| MAIN-0059 | 431 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Seven Gongdeok and Mapo stays, matched to the trip" |
| MAIN-0060 | 432 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/header[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "These are editorial starting points, not a ranking. Rates, inventory, and exact room layouts change, so compare the room you can actually book rather than the property name alone." |
| MAIN-0061 | 437 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "GONGDEOK DEFAULT" |
| MAIN-0062 | 438 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "GLAD Mapo" |
| MAIN-0063 | 439 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "AREX · subway · business · couple / small party" |
| MAIN-0064 | 442 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "GLAD Mapo is the easiest place to start if Gongdeok Station itself is the reason for choosing the area. The hotel is officially tied to Gongdeok Exits 8 and 9, with Line 5 and 6 access on one side and AREX / Gyeongui-Jungang access on the other. That makes it a sensible base for an airport arrival, a business trip, or a stay that moves between several parts of Seoul." |
| MAIN-0065 | 443 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The room range is broader than a basic business hotel. Deluxe Twin can take up to three guests with an extra bed, Jumbo Family Twin uses a double plus a single bed for up to three, and GLAD House is a larger 54.8㎡ option with a separate living area and two toilets. That gives couples and small families more flexibility than the hotel’s business image first suggests." |
| MAIN-0066 | 444 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "I would still choose it for the transport network rather than for neighborhood atmosphere. If most evenings are meant to be in Hongdae or most sightseeing is around Jongno and Myeongdong, the station convenience has to save enough time elsewhere in the itinerary to justify staying here." |

| MAIN-0075 | 464 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "LOTTE City Hotel Mapo becomes more interesting when a standard double or twin room is not enough. | MAIN-0076 | 465 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The business side is still real. | MAIN-0077 | 466 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "The catch is that the larger-room advantage belongs to specific suite categories, not the whole hotel. | MAIN-0086 | 486 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Roynet is the stronger choice when a business trip lasts long enough for the room and hotel facilities to matter. | MAIN-0087 | 487 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The AREX connection is straightforward but the exit detail matters. That is the kind of difference worth knowing before arriving with a large suitcase." |
| MAIN-0088 | 488 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "For a simple one- or two-night business trip, Roynet may offer more facilities than you actually need. | MAIN-0095 | 504 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Shilla Stay Mapo" |
| MAIN-0096 | 505 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Business · airport bus · short stay · Yeouido access" |
| MAIN-0097 | 508 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Shilla Stay Mapo is the more conventional business-hotel choice. The hotel describes itself as a premium business property near Yeouido, with a meeting room, business corner, gym and restaurant. Gongdeok Station Exit 1 is officially about a three-minute walk away, so the location works well for a short work trip where the room is mainly a reliable base between meetings." |
| MAIN-0098 | 509 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The airport options are unusually broad for this group. The hotel officially lists airport limousine buses 6015, 6701 and 6702 at the Gongdeok Station stop, in addition to AREX access through Gongdeok. That gives business travelers a choice between rail and bus depending on their flight time, luggage and terminal." |
| MAIN-0099 | 510 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Standard and Deluxe rooms are 21.7㎡ and generally designed for one or two adults, although the hotel also lists a Standard Family Twin and allows up to three guests in some rooms when a child is included. | MAIN-0105 | 525 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "FAMILY / GROUP BETWEEN MAPO & GONGDEOK" |
| MAIN-0106 | 526 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Garden Hotel" |
| MAIN-0107 | 527 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Family · group · 3–4 people · Mapo + Gongdeok" |
| MAIN-0108 | 530 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Seoul Garden Hotel earns its place by sitting between two useful stations rather than committing to just one. The hotel’s official directions put Mapo Station Exit 3 about three minutes away and Gongdeok Station Exit 8 about five minutes away. That gives you Line 5 at Mapo, while Gongdeok adds AREX, Line 6, and the Gyeongui-Jungang Line when you need them." |
| MAIN-0109 | 531 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The room mix is the real reason to look at it for families and groups. The hotel currently lists Triple rooms with three single beds, Family Twin rooms with one double and one single, and Quad rooms with a double plus a bunk bed. All three are around 25.87㎡, so the distinction is not huge room size but having bed layouts that actually match three or four travelers." |
| MAIN-0110 | 532 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "I would choose Seoul Garden when the group setup matters more than staying directly above Gongdeok Station. If two adults only need a straightforward airport-and-subway base, GLAD or Shilla Stay may be simpler. For three or four people trying to avoid booking two rooms, Seoul Garden has a clearer reason to stay on the list." |

| ATTR-0021 | 538 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[2]/@aria-label | "View Seoul Garden Hotel on Trip.com" |
| MAIN-0114 | 538 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[5]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[2]/text()[1] | "Trip.com" |

| MAIN-0116 | 547 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[6]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "HAN RIVER LUXURY STAY" |
| MAIN-0117 | 548 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[6]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Hotel Naru Seoul MGallery Ambassador" |
| MAIN-0118 | 549 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[6]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Couple · family up to 4 · luxury · Han River" |
| MAIN-0119 | 552 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[6]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Hotel Naru changes the reason for staying in this part of Mapo. This is not the hotel I would pick simply to maximize rail connections; its strongest case is the hotel itself. The official site positions it directly by the Han River, with river-view rooms, restaurant and bar facilities, and an infinity pool, while Mapo Station Exit 4 is about a five-minute walk away." |
| MAIN-0120 | 553 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[6]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Families are not limited to booking two rooms. The official Family Room is 43㎡ with two double beds and accommodates up to four guests, including children. That makes Hotel Naru a real four-person option in a category where many upscale Seoul rooms still top out at two or three." |
| MAIN-0121 | 554 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[6]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "The compromise is transport. Gongdeok’s AREX and multi-line interchange are not directly underneath the hotel; the official route from Incheon Airport suggests changing at Gongdeok and continuing one stop on Line 5 to Mapo. K-Limousine 6702 also serves the hotel, so airport access is still workable, just different from staying at Gongdeok Station itself." |
| MAIN-0122 | 555 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[6]/div[2]/p[4]/text()[1] | "That is why I would keep Hotel Naru for travelers who want a quieter, higher-end stay by the river and are willing to trade some interchange convenience for the hotel experience." |

| ATTR-0023 | 559 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[6]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/@aria-label | "Booking links for Hotel Naru Seoul MGallery Ambassador" |

| MAIN-0128 | 570 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "PRIVATE 3-BEDROOM GROUP STAY" |
| MAIN-0129 | 571 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok Stay Masil" |
| MAIN-0130 | 572 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Family · friends · 5–6 people · airport access · private stay" |
| MAIN-0131 | 575 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok Stay Masil is the clearest alternative to booking two or three hotel rooms. The entire villa has three bedrooms and a living area, and current Booking listings allow up to six guests with three double beds. It also has a kitchen and washing machine, so the layout suits families or friends who want to stay together rather than split across separate hotel rooms." |
| MAIN-0132 | 576 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "The location is genuinely useful for this kind of group trip. Seoul Stay lists the property about a three-minute walk from Gongdeok Station Exit 10, with AREX, Lines 5 and 6, and the Gyeongui-Jungang Line all available from the station." |
| MAIN-0133 | 577 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "There are two booking details I would check before choosing it. First, Seoul Stay says additional bedrooms are opened according to the number of guests: Bedroom 2 from three guests and Bedroom 3 from five guests. Second, the property has one bathroom. That may be perfectly manageable for a family, but six adults getting ready for an early flight or a full sightseeing day may feel very different." |
| MAIN-0134 | 579 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "CHECK RATES" |

| ATTR-0027 | 581 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/@aria-label | "Booking links for Gongdeok Stay Masil" |
| ATTR-0028 | 582 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[1]/@aria-label | "View Gongdeok Stay Masil on Trip.com" |
| MAIN-0136 | 582 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[1]/text()[1] | "Trip.com" |
| ATTR-0029 | 583 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[2]/@aria-label | "View Gongdeok Stay Masil on Agoda" |
| MAIN-0137 | 583 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/div[1]/article[7]/div[2]/div[1]/div[1]/a[2]/text()[1] | "Agoda" |
| MAIN-0138 | 589 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/p[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Research checked:" |
| MAIN-0139 | 589 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[6]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "September 2026. Transport details were checked against Airport Railroad and Seoul Metropolitan Government information. Hotel roles use official hotel room and facility pages where available; Gongdeok Stay Masil details use the official Visit Seoul listing. Conditions can change, so confirm the exact room and route before booking." |
| MAIN-0140 | 596 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Getting Around from Gongdeok" |
| MAIN-0141 | 600 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "AREX is convenient — but it is the All-Stop Train" |
| MAIN-0142 | 602 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok is directly on the AREX airport railroad, so you can travel between Incheon Airport and your hotel area without first going to Seoul Station. The important detail is that AREX Express does not stop at Gongdeok. The Express runs between Incheon Airport and Seoul Station, while Gongdeok is served by the All-Stop service." |
| MAIN-0143 | 603 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[1]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For most travelers that is not a problem. It simply means Gongdeok is better understood as a convenient airport connection than as an express-airport terminal." |
| MAIN-0144 | 607 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Four rail lines are useful only if your itinerary uses them" |
| MAIN-0145 | 609 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok connects Line 5, Line 6, the Gyeongui-Jungang Line and AREX. That combination is why the area can work for trips that move between the airport, Yeouido, central Seoul and Hongdae instead of concentrating on one neighborhood." |
| MAIN-0146 | 610 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[2]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "But do not choose Gongdeok because “four lines must be better than one.” If most of your trip stays in the same part of Seoul, a hotel closer to that area may still save more time." |
| MAIN-0147 | 614 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok Station itself is large" |
| MAIN-0148 | 616 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[3]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "The interchange is more substantial than a normal neighborhood subway stop. That matters when comparing hotels around different exits. A property can be close to Gongdeok on a map but still put you on the wrong side of the station for the line you use most." |
| MAIN-0149 | 620 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[1]/h3[1]/text()[1] | "Mapo Station is simpler" |
| MAIN-0150 | 622 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Mapo Station works differently. It is mainly useful as a Line 5 base, while Gongdeok is the interchange." |
| MAIN-0151 | 623 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[7]/div[1]/div[1]/article[4]/div[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "That is why Seoul Garden Hotel or Hotel Naru can still be good choices even though they are better described as Mapo Station hotels. If AREX is part of almost every airport journey, Gongdeok is easier. If the hotel itself, family room or Han River setting matters more, one extra Line 5 stop may be an acceptable exchange." |
| MAIN-0152 | 633 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "How Gongdeok Fits Into a Real Seoul Trip" |
| MAIN-0153 | 638 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Arriving from Incheon and staying in Seoul" |
| MAIN-0154 | 639 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "If you arrive at Incheon Airport and plan to spend several days moving between different parts of Seoul, Gongdeok can be a convenient first base. You can take the AREX All-Stop train directly to the neighborhood, check in, and still have Lines 5 and 6 available for the rest of the stay." |
| MAIN-0155 | 640 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "This works especially well when airport access matters but you do not need the nightlife and crowds of Hongdae outside the hotel every evening." |
| MAIN-0156 | 643 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "A business trip split between Yeouido and central Seoul" |
| MAIN-0157 | 644 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok is a practical compromise when meetings are spread between Yeouido, central Seoul, and other business areas rather than concentrated in one district." |
| MAIN-0158 | 645 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/p[3]/text()[1] | "In that situation, GLAD Mapo, Roynet, or Shilla Stay can be more useful than choosing a tourist neighborhood and commuting out of it every morning. If all of your meetings are in Gangnam, however, Gongdeok loses much of that advantage." |
| MAIN-0159 | 648 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Visiting Hongdae without sleeping in Hongdae" |
| MAIN-0160 | 649 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Some travelers want Hongdae for dinner, cafés, shopping, or an evening out—but not necessarily for the whole stay." |
| MAIN-0161 | 650 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok lets you keep Hongdae close while coming back to a quieter business-and-residential area at night." |
| MAIN-0162 | 653 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/p[1]/text()[1] | "A family or group using one base" |
| MAIN-0163 | 654 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For three or four people, Seoul Garden and the larger LOTTE City Hotel room types can solve the trip within a conventional hotel. For five or six, Gongdeok Stay Masil offers a different setup with separate bedrooms in one private stay." |
| MAIN-0164 | 655 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/p[3]/text()[1] | "The right choice is less about which property is closest to the station and more about whether the room arrangement actually works for the group." |
| MAIN-0165 | 658 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/p[1]/text()[1] | "A first or final night before the airport" |
| MAIN-0166 | 659 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/p[2]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok can also make sense for the first or final night of a Korea trip when airport access is unusually important." |
| MAIN-0167 | 660 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[8]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/p[3]/text()[1] | "But I would not move hotels just to save one airport journey. If you are already staying somewhere that works well for the rest of Seoul, changing to Gongdeok for a single night only makes sense when the transfer genuinely simplifies the schedule." |
| MAIN-0168 | 670 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "What to Check Before You Book" |
| MAIN-0169 | 672 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check the exact room type, not just the hotel name." |
| MAIN-0170 | 672 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[1]/text()[1] | "A room that allows four people may still mean two double beds in a compact space. For families and groups, check the actual bed configuration before comparing prices." |
| MAIN-0171 | 673 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check which station you will really use." |
| MAIN-0172 | 673 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[2]/text()[1] | "A Mapo Station hotel can still work well, but it is not the same as staying beside Gongdeok’s AREX and multi-line interchange. If airport rail is important, compare the full route rather than straight-line distance." |
| MAIN-0173 | 674 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check the arrival route with luggage." |
| MAIN-0174 | 674 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[3]/text()[1] | "Gongdeok is a large interchange and different exits suit different hotels. A few extra minutes may matter more with several suitcases than with a backpack." |
| MAIN-0175 | 675 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "Check late-arrival arrangements for smaller stays." |
| MAIN-0176 | 675 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[4]/text()[1] | "Full-service hotels are generally straightforward, but a private stay such as Gongdeok Stay Masil should be checked for its exact arrival and access procedure before a late flight." |
| MAIN-0177 | 676 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/strong[1]/text()[1] | "For families and groups, check bathrooms as well as bedrooms." |
| MAIN-0178 | 676 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[9]/div[1]/div[1]/ol[1]/li[5]/text()[1] | "A property that sleeps six is not automatically comfortable for six. Bed layout, bathroom count and shared living space can matter more than the advertised maximum occupancy." |
| MAIN-0179 | 685 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Compare Gongdeok With Other Seoul Bases" |
| ATTR-0030 | 687 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/@aria-label | "Related Seoul accommodation guides" |
| MAIN-0180 | 688 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[1]/span[1]/text()[1] | "Where to Stay in Myeongdong" |
| MAIN-0181 | 688 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[1]/span[2]/text()[1] | "For central sightseeing, shopping, and a simpler first trip." |
| MAIN-0182 | 689 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[2]/span[1]/text()[1] | "Where to Stay in Hongdae" |
| MAIN-0183 | 689 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[2]/span[2]/text()[1] | "For nightlife, late cafés, and staying inside the neighborhood." |
| MAIN-0184 | 690 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[3]/span[1]/text()[1] | "Hotels Near Seoul Station" |
| MAIN-0185 | 690 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[3]/span[2]/text()[1] | "For KTX, AREX Express, and rail-focused first or final nights." |
| MAIN-0186 | 691 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[4]/span[1]/text()[1] | "Best Areas for First-Time Visitors" |
| MAIN-0187 | 691 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[4]/span[2]/text()[1] | "For comparing the easiest sightseeing bases across Seoul." |
| MAIN-0188 | 692 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[5]/span[1]/text()[1] | "Hongdae vs Myeongdong" |
| MAIN-0189 | 692 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[10]/div[1]/nav[1]/a[5]/span[2]/text()[1] | "For choosing between nightlife and central first-trip convenience." |
| MAIN-0190 | 700 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Final Recommendation" |
| MAIN-0191 | 703 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "If you want a straightforward hotel directly tied to Gongdeok’s transport network, start with GLAD Mapo." |
| MAIN-0192 | 704 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[2]/text()[1] | "For a longer business stay or when a larger room matters, compare LOTTE City Hotel Mapo and Roynet Hotel Seoul Mapo. Shilla Stay Mapo is the simpler choice for a shorter business trip where airport and subway access matter more than extra room space." |
| MAIN-0193 | 705 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[3]/text()[1] | "Families and groups should look beyond the nearest station entrance. Seoul Garden Hotel has practical three- and four-person room layouts, while Gongdeok Stay Masil is the stronger option when five or six people want separate bedrooms in one private stay." |
| MAIN-0194 | 706 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[4]/text()[1] | "Choose Hotel Naru Seoul MGallery Ambassador for a different reason: the Han River setting and the hotel experience itself. If that matters more than having Gongdeok’s interchange directly underneath you, staying by Mapo Station is a reasonable compromise." |
| MAIN-0195 | 707 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[11]/div[1]/div[1]/p[5]/text()[1] | "If you are choosing Gongdeok only because the airport train stops there, compare the rest of your Seoul itinerary first. The area works best when its transport connections solve more than one part of the trip." |
| MAIN-0196 | 715 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/header[1]/h2[1]/text()[1] | "Hotels Near Gongdeok Station FAQ" |
| MAIN-0197 | 719 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Is Gongdeok a good area to stay in Seoul?" |
| MAIN-0198 | 720 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[1]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Yes, especially when airport access, business travel, or moving between several parts of Seoul matters. For a first trip focused mainly on palaces, shopping and central sightseeing, Myeongdong or Jongno may be more convenient." |
| MAIN-0199 | 723 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Does the AREX Express stop at Gongdeok?" |
| MAIN-0200 | 724 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[2]/p[1]/text()[1] | "No. Gongdeok is served by the AREX All-Stop Train. The Express service runs between Incheon Airport and Seoul Station." |
| MAIN-0201 | 727 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Is Gongdeok better than Hongdae for accommodation?" |
| MAIN-0202 | 728 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[3]/p[1]/text()[1] | "It depends on the trip. Hongdae is better when nightlife, cafés and neighborhood atmosphere are priorities. Gongdeok is quieter and stronger as a transport and business base." |
| MAIN-0203 | 731 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Should I stay near Gongdeok Station or Mapo Station?" |
| MAIN-0204 | 732 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[4]/p[1]/text()[1] | "Choose Gongdeok when AREX and multiple rail lines are important. Mapo Station can work better when you prefer a particular hotel, family room, or the Han River side and do not need the Gongdeok interchange every day." |
| MAIN-0205 | 735 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/summary[1]/text()[1] | "Is Gongdeok good for families?" |
| MAIN-0206 | 736 | /html[1]/body[1]/main[1]/section[12]/div[1]/div[1]/details[5]/p[1]/text()[1] | "It can be. LOTTE City Hotel Mapo, Seoul Garden Hotel, Hotel Naru and Gongdeok Stay Masil cover different family and group sizes. Check the exact beds, occupancy and bathroom setup rather than assuming every room works equally well for a family." |
